<h3 id="335">[335] Optimal FALQON for Quantum Approximate Optimization via Layer-wise Parameter Tuning</h3>

<h3 id="336">[336] CDS4RAG: Cyclic Dual-Sequential Hyperparameter Optimization for RAG</h3>

<h3 id="337">[337] Sanity Checks for Long-Form Hallucination Detection</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.08346](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08346) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Geigh Zollicoffer, Minh Vu, Hongli Zhan, Raymond Li, Manish Bhattarai
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI

<h3 id="338">[338] NeuroGAN-3D: Enhancing Intrinsic Functional Brain Networks via High-Fidelity 3D Generative Super-Resolution</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.08373](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08373) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: M. Moein Esfahani, Sepehr Salem Ghahfarokhi, Mohammed Alser, Jingyu Liu, Vince Calhoun
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI

<h3 id="339">[339] Reinforcement Learning for Scalable and Trustworthy Intelligent Systems</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.08378](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08378) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Guangchen Lan
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: Reinforcement Learning, Federated Learning, LLM Alignment
- **Summary**: 本博士论文解决了强化学习面临的两个核心挑战：在通信带宽受限的分布式环境中的可扩展性，以及LLM后训练中的偏好对齐和安全要求。研究涵盖联邦优化、偏好对齐和上下文安全等四个互补贡献。

<h3 id="340">[340] What Software Engineering Looks Like to AI Agents? -- An Empirical Study of AI-Only Technical Discourse on MoltBook</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.08380](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08380) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Junyu Huo, Ziqi Mao, Zihao Wan, Gouri Ginde
- **Subjects**: cs.SE; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Multi-Agent System, Social Network Analysis
- **Summary**: 本文研究了AI代理在纯AI社交网络MoltBook中的技术交流行为，通过分析4,707篇技术帖子并与GitHub Discussions上的人类开发者讨论进行对比。研究发现AI代理的技术讨论主要集中在安全与信任、记忆与上下文管理、工具与API、调试与错误处理等主题，但缺乏人类开发者讨论中常见的具体代码、环境细节、运行时错误和复现步骤等上下文信息。

<h3 id="341">[341] Decoupling Endpoint and Semantic Transition Learning for Zero-Shot Composed Image Retrieval</h3>

<h3 id="342">[342] AIPO: : Learning to Reason from Active Interaction</h3>

<h3 id="343">[343] Built Environment Reasoning from Remote Sensing Imagery Using Large Vision--Language Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.08404](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08404) (cross-listed)

<h3 id="344">[344] Effective Explanations Support Planning Under Uncertainty</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.08406](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08406) (cross-listed)

<h3 id="345">[345] Mechanism Design Is Not Enough: Prosocial Agents for Cooperative AI</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.08426](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08426) (cross-listed)

<h3 id="346">[346] A Semantic-Sampling Framework for Evaluating Calibration in Open-Ended Question Answering</h3>

<h3 id="347">[347] A meshfree exterior calculus for generalizable and data-efficient learning of physics from point clouds</h3>

<h3 id="348">[348] Magis-Bench: Evaluating LLMs on Magistrate-Level Legal Tasks</h3>

- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI

- **Summary**: 本文引入Magis-Bench基准，用于评估LLM在巴西司法考试法官级别任务上的表现。结果显示即使最佳模型得分也低于70%，表明司法级别的法律推理和写作对当前LLM仍具挑战性。

<h3 id="349">[349] DUET: Optimize Token-Budget Allocation for Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards</h3>

<h3 id="350">[350] Defense effectiveness across architectural layers: a mechanistic evaluation of persistent memory attacks on stateful LLM agents</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.08442](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08442) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Jun Wen Leong
- **Subjects**: cs.CR; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: LLM Security, LLM Agent, RAG
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/junwenleong/stateful-agent-security-eval)
- **Summary**: 本文系统评估了六种防御机制在四个架构层面对LLM代理持久记忆攻击的有效性。结果表明输入级和检索级防御基本失效，而内存层的Memory Sandbox通过移除攻击所需的召回能力，在大多数模型上实现0%攻击成功率。

<h3 id="351">[351] Zero-shot Imitation Learning by Latent Topology Mapping</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.08450](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08450) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Maxwell J. Jacobson, Yexiang Xue
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Imitation Learning, Zero-Shot Learning
- **Summary**: 本文提出ZALT模仿学习方法，通过识别轨迹汇聚或发散的潜在枢纽状态，学习枢纽间转移的策略和动力学模型，并在枢纽拓扑上规划来完成未见任务。在3D迷宫环境中，ZALT实现55%的零样本成功率，远超最强基线的6%。

<h3 id="352">[352] Sink vs. diagonal patterns as mechanisms for attention switch and oversmoothing prevention</h3>

- **Summary**: 本文分析注意力机制中sink和对角模式作为注意力切换和防止过度平滑机制的作用。作者证明了sink与硬注意力切换的等价性，并定量比较了表示sink与对角模式的代价，解释了预训练transformer为何偏好sink模式。

<h3 id="353">[353] Recovering Physical Dynamics from Discrete Observations via Intrinsic Differential Consistency</h3>

<h3 id="354">[354] When Child Inherits: Modeling and Exploiting Subagent Spawn in Multi-Agent Networks</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.08460](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08460) (cross-listed)

- **Subjects**: cs.CR; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, LLM Security, Multi-Agent System
- **Summary**: 本文从子智能体继承的角度分析了多智能体LLM网络的安全风险，发现当前框架可能通过不安全的内存继承、弱资源控制等方式违反信任边界。作者展示了这些风险并提出基于安全不变量的防御措施。

<h3 id="355">[355] Do Benchmarks Underestimate LLM Performance? Evaluating Hallucination Detection With LLM-First Human-Adjudicated Assessment</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.08462](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08462) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: I. F. Atasoy, B. Mutlu, E. A. Sezer, A. Wahdan
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Hallucination, LLM Evaluation, Benchmark
- **Venue**: ECIR 2026 Workshop
- **Summary**: 本研究通过人工仲裁重新评估了幻觉检测基准（QAGS-C和SummEval），发现LLM的判断往往比原始人工标注更符合仲裁结果。结果表明对于易产生歧义的任务，单轮标注可能不足，模型辅助的重新评估能产生更可靠的基准。

<h3 id="356">[356] PYTHALAB-MERA: Validation-Grounded Memory, Retrieval, and Acceptance Control for Frozen-LLM Coding Agents</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.08468](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08468) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Mehmet Iscan
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Code Generation, LLM Agent, Reinforcement Learning
- **Summary**: 本文提出了PYTHALAB-MERA，一个用于冻结LLM的外部控制器，提供验证驱动的情景记忆和自适应检索-动作选择。该系统使用TD(lambda)风格的资格迹传播延迟信用，在强化学习编码任务上显著优于自精炼基线。

<h3 id="357">[357] Transformers Can Implement Preconditioned Richardson Iteration for In-Context Gaussian Kernel Regression</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.08475](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08475) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Mingsong Yan, Dongyang Li, Charles Kulick, Sui Tang
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; math.NA; math.OC
- **Tags**: In-Context Learning, Deep Learning Theory, Transformer Architecture
- **Summary**: 本文从理论上证明了标准softmax注意力Transformer可以通过实现预条件Richardson迭代来完成高斯核的上下文核岭回归。作者构造了一个对数深度的Transformer实现ε精度预测，并通过实验验证了这一机制解释。

<h3 id="358">[358] MathConstraint: Automated Generation of Verified Combinatorial Reasoning Instances for LLMs</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.08498](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08498) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Viresh Pati, Zhengyu Li, Piyush Jha, Rahul Garg, Yatharth Sejpal, Vijay Ganesh
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.LO
- **Tags**: LLM Reasoning, Benchmark, Mathematical Reasoning
- **Summary**: 本文介绍了MathConstraint，一个用于评估LLM组合推理能力的自适应基准，使用约束满足问题和求解器验证。基准包含可参数化的问题类型，能够随模型能力提升而扩展难度，揭示了工具访问对性能的显著影响。

<h3 id="359">[359] Multi-Level Graph Attention Network Contrastive Learning for Knowledge-Aware Recommendation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.08499](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08499) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Zhifei Hu, Feng Xia
- **Subjects**: cs.IR; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Recommender System, Graph Neural Network, Knowledge Graph
- **Summary**: 本文提出了一个多视图图对比学习框架用于知识感知推荐，通过多级自监督对比学习（层间、层内、交互级）解决标签稀疏和知识图谱噪声问题。实验表明该方法在多个公开数据集上优于现有方法。

<h3 id="360">[360] Scaling Limits of Long-Context Transformers</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.08505](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08505) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Giuseppe Bruno, Shi Chen, Zhengjiang Lin, Yury Polyanskiy, Philippe Rigollet
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; math.PR; math.ST
- **Tags**: Long Context, Deep Learning Theory, Attention Mechanism
- **Summary**: 本文研究了长上下文Transformer中softmax自注意力的缩放极限，刻画了不同温度区间下注意力权重和输出的极限分布。分析揭示了临界缩放律，并表明亚临界情况下的注意力近似实现了一个反向热方程。

<h3 id="361">[361] A Single Neuron Is Sufficient to Bypass Safety Alignment in Large Language Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.08513](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08513) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Hamid Kazemi, Atoosa Chegini, Maria Safi
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: LLM Alignment, LLM Security, Interpretability
- **Summary**: 本文证明了LLM的安全对齐可以通过针对单个神经元来绕过——抑制拒绝神经元可绕过安全检查，放大概念神经元可诱导有害内容。该漏洞在7个模型（1.7B到70B参数）上得到验证，无需任何训练或提示工程。

<h3 id="362">[362] MARLaaS: Multi-Tenant Asynchronous Reinforcement Learning as a Service</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.08527](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08527) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Timothy Tin Long Yu, Gursimran Singh, Ge Shi, Hanieh Sadri, Yong Zhang, Zhenan Fan
- **Subjects**: cs.DC; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Reinforcement Learning, LLM Training, Distributed Training
- **Summary**: 本文提出了MARLaaS，一个支持多租户异步强化学习微调服务的系统。通过共享基础模型和LoRA适配器，以及解耦的异步架构，该系统在多任务设置下实现了高达4.3倍的加速器利用率提升和85%的训练时间减少。

<h3 id="363">[363] Continuity Laws for Sequential Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.08539](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08539) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Annan Yu, Dongwei Lyu, N. Benjamin Erichson
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: State Space Model, Deep Learning Theory, Time Series Analysis
- **Summary**: 本文将序列模型的时间连续性形式化为时间细化下的收敛性，研究了S4和S6（Mamba）等模型的连续性行为。研究发现任务连续性、模型连续性和模型性能之间存在明显的经验关联，连续性还支持时间子采样策略。

<h3 id="364">[364] VeriContest: A Competitive-Programming Benchmark for Verifiable Code Generation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.08553](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08553) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Zichen Xie, Mrigank Pawagi, Yuxin Liu, Aaditi Rai, Lize Shao, John Berberian Jr., Sicong Che, Wenxi Wang
- **Subjects**: cs.SE; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Code Generation, Program Verification, Benchmark
- **Summary**: 本文提出了VeriContest，一个包含946道竞赛编程问题的可验证代码生成基准，支持Rust和Verus的形式化规范与证明。评估揭示了代码生成能力（92%）与端到端可验证合成（5.29%）之间的巨大差距。

<h3 id="365">[365] MC-RFM: Geometry-Aware Few-Shot Adaptation via Mixed-Curvature Riemannian Flow Matching</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.08557](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08557) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Salim Khazem, Ibrahim Mohamed Serouis, Zakaria Ezzahed
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Few-Shot Learning, Flow Matching, Transfer Learning
- **Summary**: 本文提出了MC-RFM，一个混合曲率黎曼流匹配框架用于冻结视觉骨干网络的少样本适应。该方法将适应特征表示在结合双曲因子和欧几里得因子的乘积流形上，在多数评估设置中取得最佳性能。

<h3 id="366">[366] ZAYA1-VL-8B Technical Report</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.08560](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08560) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Hassan Shapourian, Kasra Hejazi, Olabode M. Sule, Beren Millidge
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Vision-Language Model, Mixture-of-Experts, Multimodal Learning
- **Summary**: 本文介绍了ZAYA1-VL-8B，一个紧凑的混合专家视觉语言模型，采用视觉特定LoRA适配器和图像token双向注意力。该模型在仅有14亿活跃参数的情况下，在图像理解、推理和计数基准上达到与更大模型竞争的性能。

<h3 id="367">[367] Uncovering Intra-expert Activation Sparsity for Efficient Mixture-of-Expert Model Execution</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.08575](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08575) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Jongseok Park, Sunga Kim, Zhenyu Gu, Ion Stoica, Alvin Cheung
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Mixture-of-Experts, Model Compression, LLM Inference
- **Summary**: 本文探索并利用MoE模型中的专家内激活稀疏性，发现现有预训练模型中存在高达90%的专家内稀疏性且不损失精度。作者扩展了vLLM以利用这种稀疏性，在MoE层执行上实现了高达2.5倍的加速。

<h3 id="368">[368] Probing the Impact of Scale on Data-Efficient, Generalist Transformer World Models for Atari</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.08578](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08578) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Jooyeon Kim
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: World Model, Reinforcement Learning, Model-Based RL
- **Summary**: 本文分析了Transformer世界模型在Atari 100k基准上的缩放行为，发现不同环境具有不同的缩放特性。联合训练多个环境可以稳定缩放动态，确保所有环境的单调增益，改进的保真度直接转化为下游控制性能提升。

<h3 id="369">[369] Slipstream: Trajectory-Grounded Compaction Validation for Long-Horizon Agents</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.08580](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08580) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Zhuofu Chen, Rui Pan, Yinwei Dai, Ravi Netravali
- **Subjects**: cs.MA; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Long Context
- **Summary**: 本文针对长时序LLM智能体的上下文压缩问题，提出了Slipstream系统，通过异步压缩和轨迹验证来保持智能体的推理意图和关键约束，在编码和网页浏览任务上显著提升了准确率并降低了延迟。

<h3 id="370">[370] PromptDx: Differentiable Prompt Tuning for Multimodal In-Context Alzheimer's Diagnosis</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.08585](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08585) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Lujia Zhong, Yihao Xia, Shuo Huang, Jianwei Zhang, Yonggang Shi
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Medical AI, In-Context Learning, Multimodal Learning
- **Summary**: 本文提出了PromptDx框架，通过可微分提示调优机制将多模态表示与预训练的TabPFN上下文学习引擎对齐，实现了阿尔茨海默病的诊断，仅用1%的上下文样本即可达到优越性能。

<h3 id="371">[371] Kaczmarz Linear Attention</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.08587](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08587) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Jiaxuan Zou, Ruifeng Ren, Yong Liu
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Long Context, Attention Mechanism, Transformer Architecture
- **Summary**: 本文提出了Kaczmarz线性注意力(KLA)，通过键范数归一化的动态步长改进了Gated DeltaNet的残差更新，在保持线性复杂度的同时实现了更低的困惑度和更好的检索性能。

<h3 id="372">[372] Causal Stories from Sensor Traces: Auditing Epistemic Overreach in LLM-Generated Personal Sensing Explanations</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.08590](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08590) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Shanshan Zhu, Han Zhang, J. Doris Chi, Subigya Nepal, Koustuv Saha
- **Subjects**: cs.HC; cs.AI; cs.CL; cs.CY
- **Tags**: LLM Evaluation, LLM Hallucination
- **Summary**: 本文研究了LLM在解释个人感知数据时存在的"认知过度"(Epistemic Overreach)问题，即生成的解释超出了可用证据所能支持的范围。作者在三个大学生感知数据集上测试了三种LLM系列，发现模型经常在缺乏数据支持的情况下做出因果归因，即使提供更多上下文或明确约束提示也无法完全消除这一问题。

<h3 id="373">[373] Lattice Deduction Transformers</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.08605](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08605) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Liam Davis, Leopold Haller, Alberto Alfarano, Mark Santolucito
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.LO
- **Tags**: LLM Reasoning, Logical Reasoning, Transformer Architecture
- **Summary**: 本文提出了格点演绎变换器(LDT)，一种通过在前向传播之间将隐状态投影到格点上来近似逻辑演绎的循环变换器，在数独和迷宫基准测试上实现了高准确率并保持逻辑正确性。

<h3 id="374">[374] Reasoning-Aware Training for Time Series Forecasting</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.08625](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08625) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Md Atik Ahamed, Mihir Parmar, Palash Goyal, Chun-Liang Li, Qiang Cheng, Tomas Pfister, Jinsung Yoon
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Time Series Forecasting, Knowledge Distillation, Foundation Model
- **Summary**: 本文提出了STRIDE框架，通过将LLM推理蒸馏到连续嵌入空间来增强时间序列基础模型的预测能力和可解释性，在多个基准测试上实现了最先进的数值预测性能。

<h3 id="375">[375] PARD-2: Target-Aligned Parallel Draft Model for Dual-Mode Speculative Decoding</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.08632](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08632) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Zihao An, Taichi Liu, Ziqiong Liu, Dong Li, Ruofeng Liu, Emad Barsoum
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Speculative Decoding, LLM Inference
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/AMD-AGI/PARD)
- **Summary**: 本文提出了PARD-2框架，通过置信度自适应令牌优化将草稿模型的目标与接受长度对齐，实现了高达6.94倍的无损加速，同时支持目标依赖和目标独立两种模式。

<h3 id="376">[376] Geometry Guided Self-Consistency for Physical AI</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.08638](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08638) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yinwei Dai, Zhuofu Chen, Lijie Yang, Ravi Netravali
- **Subjects**: cs.RO; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Embodied AI, Diffusion Model, Robotics
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/dywsjtu/keystone)
- **Summary**: 本文提出了KeyStone方法，通过在连续动作空间中对候选动作轨迹进行聚类并选择最大簇的中心点，实现了扩散模型动作生成的自一致性推理，显著提升了物理AI任务的成功率。

<h3 id="377">[377] AgentCollabBench: Diagnosing When Good Agents Make Bad Collaborators</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.08647](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08647) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Aritra Mazumder, Shubhashis Roy Dipta, Nusrat Jahan Lia, Tanzila Khan, Kainat Raisa Hossain, Nehaa Shri, Shubhrangshu Debsarkar, Humayra Tasnim, Gour Gupal Talukder Shawon, Debjoty Mitra, Sumaiya Ahmed Rani, Al Jami Islam Anik, Al Nafeu Khan
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Multi-Agent System, LLM Evaluation, Benchmark
- **Summary**: 本文引入了AgentCollabBench基准测试，用于诊断多智能体系统中的行为风险，包括指令衰减、错误信念传播、上下文泄漏等问题，揭示了通信拓扑是影响多跳信息存活的主要风险因素。

<h3 id="378">[378] Privacy-Aware Video Anomaly Detection through Orthogonal Subspace Projection</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.08651](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08651) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Lei Wang, Wenxiang Diao, Andrew Busch, Jun Zhou, Yongsheng Gao
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Anomaly Detection, Privacy, Video Understanding
- **Venue**: ICML 2026
- **Summary**: 本文提出了正交投影层(OPL)用于隐私感知的视频异常检测，通过弱监督信号抑制面部属性同时保留姿态和运动等非身份特征，在保持检测准确率的同时增强了隐私保护。

<h3 id="379">[379] Fitting Multilinear Polynomials for Logic Gate Networks</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.08657](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08657) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Youngsung Kim
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Neural Architecture, Circuit Design
- **Summary**: 本文研究了可学习逻辑门网络，提出在4维多项式空间而非16维softmax空间中工作，通过CovJac方法解决了梯度饥饿问题，在多个数据集上超越了基线Soft-Mix方法。

<h3 id="380">[380] Sketch-and-Verify: Structured Inference-Time Scaling via Program Sketching</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.08658](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08658) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Shan Jiang, Zijian Yi, Chenguang Zhu
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.SE
- **Tags**: Code Generation, LLM Inference
- **Summary**: 本文提出了SKETCHVERIFY方法，通过让LLM枚举算法策略并生成程序草图来改进代码生成，证明了在同层级模型中草图方法优于扁平采样，是成本效益更高的推理时计算策略。

<h3 id="381">[381] Explanation Fairness in Large Language Models: An Empirical Analysis of Disparities in How LLMs Justify Decisions Across Demographic Groups</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.08671](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08671) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Gautam Veldanda
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Fairness, LLM Evaluation, AI Ethics
- **Summary**: 本文引入了解释公平性分类法(EFT)来衡量LLM在不同人口群体间解释决策的差异，发现所有测量维度都存在统计显著的差异，且模型选择对差异幅度有强烈影响。

<h3 id="382">[382] Attention-based graph neural networks: a survey</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.08679](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08679) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Chengcheng Sun, Chenhao Li, Xiang Lin, Tianji Zheng, Fanrong Meng, Xiaobin Rui, Zhixiao Wang
- **Subjects**: cs.SI; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Graph Neural Network, Survey, Attention Mechanism
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/sunxiaobei/awesome-attention-based-gnns)
- **Summary**: 本文对基于注意力的图神经网络进行了全面综述，提出了两级分类法涵盖图循环注意力网络、图注意力网络和图变换器，详细回顾了各种架构的优缺点并讨论了未来方向。

<h3 id="383">[383] Semantic Voting: Execution-Grounded Consensus for LLM Code Generation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.08680](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08680) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Shan Jiang, Zijian Yi, Chenguang Zhu
- **Subjects**: cs.SE; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Code Generation, LLM Evaluation
- **Summary**: 本文研究了LLM代码生成中的执行验证选择方法，发现基于执行的选择器显著优于输出模式多数投票，且输入质量比聚合规则更重要，深层思考对执行方法可能产生负面影响。

<h3 id="384">[384] Core-Halo Decomposition: Decentralizing Large-Scale Fixed-Point Problems</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.08681](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08681) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Haixiang, Yang Xu, Jiefu Zhang, Xudong Wu, Zihan Zhou, Jun He, Jiayu Chen
- **Subjects**: stat.ML; cs.AI; cs.LG; math.NA
- **Tags**: Distributed Computing, Multi-Agent System, Optimization
- **Summary**: 本文提出了核心-光环分解方法来解决大规模不动点问题的去中心化求解，通过分离写入所有权和只读评估上下文，在保持并行化优势的同时实现了接近中心化的性能。

<h3 id="385">[385] Event Fields: Learning Latent Event Structure for Waveform Foundation Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.08685](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08685) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Li Na, Yuanyun Zhang, Shi Li
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Medical AI, Foundation Model, Self-Supervised Learning
- **Summary**: 本文提出了一类新的波形基础模型，将生理时间序列建模为潜在事件过程的实现，而非传统的序列表示。该方法通过自监督学习框架，结合分割感知编码器和潜在交互算子，在心律失常分类、血流动力学预测和波形检索等任务上取得了优于序列基线的性能。

<h3 id="386">[386] PrepBench: How Far Are We from Natural-Language-Driven Data Preparation?</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.08687](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08687) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Jingzhe Xu, Rui Wang, Jiannan Wang, Guoliang Li
- **Subjects**: cs.DB; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Vision-Language Model, Parameter-Efficient Fine-Tuning, Model Merging
- **Summary**: 本文提出了Gate-and-Merge框架，实现了视觉语言模型的零样本组合个性化，无需共现训练即可在推理时组合多个用户定义的概念。该方法通过门控机制合并概念特定的LoRA更新，有效抑制了无关激活和干扰。

<h3 id="387">[387] Structure-Centric Graph Foundation Model via Geometric Bases</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.08689](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08689) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Xiaodong He, Haolan He, Ruiyi Fang, Ming Sun, Zhao Kang
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.SI
- **Tags**: Graph Foundation Model, Transfer Learning, Representation Learning
- **Venue**: ICML 2026
- **Summary**: 本文提出了SCGFM，一种以图拓扑为核心知识来源的图基础模型，通过可学习的几何基和Gromov-Wasserstein距离实现结构对齐的潜在表示。实验表明该方法在图级和节点级任务上具有强大的域内和跨域泛化能力。

<h3 id="388">[388] Gate-and-Merge: Zero-shot Compositional Personalization of Vision Language Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.08702](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08702) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Guodong Ding, Angela Yao
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Vision-Language Model, LLM Personalization, Zero-Shot Learning
- **Summary**: 本文提出了一种名为Gate-and-Merge的零样本框架，用于视觉语言模型的组合个性化。该方法将每个概念独立学习为轻量级LoRA适配器，在推理时通过门控机制选择性地合并概念特定的更新，从而实现多个用户定义概念的联合识别或描述，无需共现训练。

<h3 id="389">[389] REAP: Reinforcement-Learning End-to-End Autonomous Parking with Gaussian Splatting Simulator for Real2Sim2Real Transfer</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.08713](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08713) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Changze Li, Zhe Chen, Shaoyu Chen, Lisen Mu, Yijian Li, Yuelong Yu, Qian Zhang, Qing Su, Ming Yang, Tong Qin
- **Subjects**: cs.RO; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Autonomous Driving, Reinforcement Learning, Sim-to-Real
- **Summary**: 本文提出了REAP方法，一种基于强化学习的端到端自动泊车框架，结合Soft Actor-Critic和3D高斯溅射模拟器实现Real2Sim2Real迁移。该方法成功处理了包括狭窄机械车位在内的极端泊车场景。

<h3 id="390">[390] AgentForesight: Online Auditing for Early Failure Prediction in Multi-Agent Systems</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.08715](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08715) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Boxuan Zhang, Jianing Zhu, Zeru Shi, Dongfang Liu, Ruixiang Tang
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.MA
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Multi-Agent System, Failure Detection
- **Summary**: 本文提出了AgentForesight框架，将多智能体系统的故障诊断重构为在线审计问题，在轨迹执行过程中预测最早的致命错误。该方法构建了AFTraj-2K语料库并训练了紧凑的审计器，性能超越GPT-4.1等商业模型。

<h3 id="391">[391] Debugging the Debuggers: Failure-Anchored Structured Recovery for Software Engineering Agents</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.08717](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08717) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Chenyu Zhao, Shenglin Zhang, Yihang Lin, Wenwei Gu, Zhimin Chen, Yongqian Sun, Dan Pei, Chetan Bansal, Saravan Rajmohan, Minghua Ma
- **Subjects**: cs.SE; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Agent Debugging, Software Engineering
- **Summary**: 本文提出了PROBE框架，用于软件工程智能体的结构化恢复，通过遥测层、诊断层和指导门将失败运行信息转化为有界的恢复指导。在仓库级修复、企业工作流恢复和AIOps场景中，该方法显著优于基线。

<h3 id="392">[392] Control Your View: High-Resolution Global Semantic Manipulation in Learned Image Compression</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.08727](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08727) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Jiaming Liang, Chi-Man Pun, Weisi Lin, Greta Seng Peng Mok
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Image Compression, Adversarial Robustness
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/chinaliangjiaming/PGD2-GSM)
- **Summary**: 本文研究了学习图像压缩系统中的高分辨率全局语义操纵攻击，揭示了现有PGD方法在处理振荡和细化阶段时的不足。作者提出了PGD²-GSM方法，通过周期性几何衰减调度首次稳定实现了高分辨率全局语义操纵。

<h3 id="393">[393] AdaPreLoRA: Adafactor Preconditioned Low-Rank Adaptation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.08734](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08734) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Ziyun Liu, Fengmiao Bian, Jian-Feng Cai
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: Parameter-Efficient Fine-Tuning, Optimization, LLM Training
- **Summary**: 本文提出了AdaPreLoRA，将Adafactor的对角Kronecker预条件器与LoRA优化相结合，通过寻找最优因子空间更新来近似预条件化的权重空间方向。实验表明该方法在保持LoRA级内存效率的同时具有竞争力。

<h3 id="394">[394] SlimQwen: Exploring the Pruning and Distillation in Large MoE Model Pre-training</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.08738](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08738) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Shengkun Tang, Zekun Wang, Bo Zheng, Liangyu Wang, Rui Men, Siqi Zhang, Xiulong Yuan, Zihan Qiu, Zhiqiang Shen, Dayiheng Liu
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: Model Compression, Knowledge Distillation, Mixture-of-Experts
- **Summary**: 本文系统研究了大规模预训练中MoE模型的压缩，发现剪枝预训练模型优于从头训练，结合知识蒸馏与语言建模损失效果更佳。作者将Qwen3-Next-80A3B压缩为23A2B模型，保持了竞争性能。

<h3 id="395">[395] Causal Dimensionality of Transformer Representations: Measurement, Scaling, and Layer Structure</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.08740](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08740) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Nilesh Sarkar, Dawar Jyoti Deka
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Interpretability, Transformer Architecture, Representation Learning
- **Summary**: 本文引入了因果维度κ来衡量Transformer残差流的有效秩，发现表示容量增长快于因果容量，形成表示-因果楔。该度量对模型缩放具有不变性，并在网络深度上呈现结构化分布。

<h3 id="396">[396] Narrative Landscape: Mapping Narrative Dispositions Across LLMs</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.08742](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08742) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Donghoon Jung, Jiwoo Choi, Songeun Chae, Seohyon Jung
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Evaluation, Benchmark
- **Venue**: NLP4DH 2026 Workshop
- **Summary**: 本文提出了一个量化框架来分析LLM的叙事倾向，通过一致性和多样性两个维度刻画模型在受控叙事任务中的稳定输出规律。研究揭示了模型家族间的刚性-探索谱系，以及指令类型对选择空间几何的影响。

<h3 id="397">[397] MeshFIM: Local Low-Poly Mesh Editing via Fill-in-the-Middle Autoregressive Generation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.08744](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08744) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Dingdong Yang, Jian Liu, Biwen Lei, Haohan Weng, Zhuo Chen, Song Guo, Hao Richard Zhang, Ali Mahdavi Amiri, Chunchao Guo
- **Subjects**: cs.GR; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: 3D Vision, Autoregressive Model, Mesh Generation
- **Summary**: 本文提出了MeshFIM框架，实现了低多边形网格的局部编辑，通过填充中间的自回归生成在保持周围上下文的同时重新生成目标区域。该方法支持交互式笔刷编辑和自动缺陷修复应用。

<h3 id="398">[398] Communicating Sound Through Natural Language</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.08750](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08750) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Emanuele Rossi, Emanuele Rodolà
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.CL; cs.MA
- **Tags**: Audio-Language Model, Speech Synthesis, LLM Application
- **Summary**: 本文提出了词汇声学编码框架，使LLM智能体能够通过自然语言传输声音，将波形分析为可解释的声学描述符并量化为英语句子。该方法将文本同时作为丰富描述和传输表示，保持了可测量的声学结构。

<h3 id="399">[399] Omni-scale Learning-based Sequential Decision Framework for Order Fulfillment of Tote-handling Robotic Systems</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.08758](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08758) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Jiaxin Liu, Peng Yang, Yuping Li, Xinyue Xie
- **Subjects**: cs.RO; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Robotics, Multi-Agent System, Reinforcement Learning
- **Summary**: 本文提出了OLSF-TRS框架，结合组合优化与多智能体强化学习，为料箱搬运机器人系统提供统一的订单履行决策。该方法在小规模场景中达到近优性能，在大规模场景中显著优于启发式基线。

<h3 id="400">[400] Unlearners Can Lie: Evaluating and Improving Honesty in LLM Unlearning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.08765](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08765) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Renjie Gu, Jiazhen Du, Yihua Zhang, Sijia Liu
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Machine Unlearning, LLM Hallucination, AI Safety
- **Venue**: ACL 2026
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/renjiegu)
- **Summary**: 本文研究了LLM遗忘中的诚实性问题，发现现有方法常产生幻觉或不一致行为。作者提出了遗忘诚实的形式化定义和ReVa表示对齐方法，显著提高了对遗忘知识的拒绝率，同时保持对保留知识的诚实性。

<h3 id="401">[401] A Reconfigurable Multiplier Architecture for Error-Resilient Applications in RISC-V Core</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.08785](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08785) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Pragun Jaswal, L. Hemanth Krishna, B. Srinivasu
- **Subjects**: cs.AR; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Hardware Architecture, Energy Efficiency, Edge Computing
- **Venue**: ISVLSI 2026
- **Summary**: 本文提出了一种集成到RISC-V核心中的运行时可重构乘法器架构，支持精确和近似计算模式，可在边缘AI应用中实现细粒度的能效控制。该设计在精确和近似模式下分别实现44%-52%和62%-68%的功耗降低，同时保持计算性能。

<h3 id="402">[402] cuRegOT: A GPU-Accelerated Solver for Entropic-Regularized Optimal Transport</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.08793](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08793) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yixuan Qiu
- **Subjects**: cs.MS; cs.AI; cs.LG; stat.CO; stat.ML
- **Tags**: Optimal Transport, GPU Computing
- **Summary**: 本文提出了cuRegOT，一个针对熵正则化最优传输的高性能GPU求解器，通过摊销符号分析策略、异步Sinkhorn迭代生成机制和融合内核等优化，显著加速了大规模最优传输计算。

<h3 id="403">[403] Deterministic Decomposition of Stochastic Generative Dynamics</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.08794](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08794) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Xingyu Song, Yuan Mei, Naoya Takeishi
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Generative Model, Flow Matching
- **Summary**: 本文提出了一种将随机生成动力学分解为传输分量和渗透分量的方法，通过Bridge Matching框架学习分解后的生成动力学，实现了可解释和可控的采样过程。

<h3 id="404">[404] PPU-Bench:Real World Benchmark for Personalized Partial Unlearning in Vision Language Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.08800](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08800) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Jiahui Guang, Zexun Zhan, Zhenlin Xu, Cuiyun Gao, Haiyan Wang, Jing Li, Zhaoquan Gu, Yanchun Zhang
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Machine Unlearning, Vision-Language Model, Benchmark
- **Summary**: 本文提出了PPU-Bench，一个针对多模态大语言模型个性化部分遗忘的真实世界基准测试，包含24K多模态样本，评估方法在移除目标知识同时保留非目标事实的能力。

<h3 id="405">[405] Curvature-Aware Captioning:Leveraging Geodesic Attention for 3D Scene Understanding</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.08808](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08808) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Ziyao He, Yingjie Liu, ZhangYangRui, Mingsong Chen, Xuan Tang, Xian Wei
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: 3D Vision, Vision-Language Model, Dense Captioning
- **Venue**: CVPR 2026
- **Summary**: 本文提出了一种曲率感知的3D场景描述框架，通过在非欧几里得空间中引入测地线注意力机制，解决了稀疏点云数据处理中的定位-语境化冲突问题，在ScanRefer和Nr3D基准上取得了最先进的性能。

<h3 id="406">[406] SimReg: Achieving Higher Performance in the Pretraining via Embedding Similarity Regularization</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.08809](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08809) (cross-listed)

- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Pre-training, Representation Learning
- **Summary**: 本文提出了SimReg，一种嵌入相似性正则化损失，通过鼓励同标签token表示更相似、不同标签token分离来改进大语言模型预训练，在密集和MoE架构上均实现了超过30%的训练收敛加速和超过1%的零样本性能提升。

<h3 id="407">[407] Compressed Video Aggregator: Content-driven Module for Efficient Micro-Video Recommendation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.08810](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08810) (cross-listed)

- **Tags**: Recommender System, Video Understanding
- **Summary**: 本文提出了压缩视频聚合器(CVA)，一个轻量级的短视频推荐模块，通过聚合冻结的视频基础模型嵌入并使用潜在推理来生成紧凑的视频嵌入，在大幅减少训练时间和GPU内存的同时实现了性能提升。

<h3 id="408">[408] FraudBench: A Multimodal Benchmark for Detecting AI-Generated Fraudulent Refund Evidence</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.08820](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08820) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Xinyu Yan, Boyang Chen, Jiaming Zhang, Tiantong Wu, Hong Xi Tae, Yichen He, Tiantong Wang, Yachun Mi, Yurong Hao, Yilei Zhao, Lei Xiao, Longtao Huang, Pengjun Xie, Wei Liu, Wei Yang Bryan Lim
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; cs.CR
- **Tags**: Benchmark, Deepfake Detection, Multimodal Learning
- **Summary**: 本文介绍了FraudBench，一个用于检测AI生成欺诈退款证据的多模态基准测试，涵盖电商、外卖和旅游服务场景，揭示了当前MLLM和专业检测器在声明条件下的欺诈证据验证方面存在显著差距。

<h3 id="409">[409] VECTOR-Drive: Tightly Coupled Vision-Language and Trajectory Expert Routing for End-to-End Autonomous Driving</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.08830](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08830) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Rui Zhao, Jianlin Yu, Zhenhai Gao, Jiaqiao Liu, Fei Gao
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; cs.RO
- **Tags**: Autonomous Driving, Vision-Language Model, Trajectory Prediction
- **Summary**: 本文提出了VECTOR-DRIVE，一个紧密耦合的视觉-语言-动作框架，通过共享自注意力保持多模态交互，同时根据token语义将前馈计算路由到视觉-语言专家或轨迹专家，在Bench2Drive上实现了88.91的驾驶分数。

<h3 id="410">[410] The Grounding Gap: How LLMs Anchor the Meaning of Abstract Concepts Differently from Humans</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.08837](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08837) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Odysseas S. Chlapanis, Orfeas Menis Mastromichalakis, Christos H. Papadimitriou
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Evaluation, Cognitive Science
- **Summary**: 本文通过复制认知科学中的属性生成实验，研究了21个前沿和开源LLM如何锚定抽象概念，发现模型过度依赖词汇关联而低估情感和内部状态相关属性，与人类响应的相关性最高仅为r=0.37，揭示了显著的接地差距。

<h3 id="411">[411] Generating Leakage-Free Benchmarks for Robust RAG Evaluation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.08838](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08838) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Jiayi Liu, Jiaxing Zhang, Bowen Jin, Jennifer Neville
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: RAG, Benchmark
- **Summary**: 本文提出了SeedRG，一个半合成基准生成管道，通过从问题-上下文对中提取推理图并通过类型约束实体替换生成新样本，解决了RAG评估中的知识泄漏问题，确保生成的基准测试需要检索才能解答。

<h3 id="412">[412] Geometrically Constrained Stenosis Editing in Coronary Angiography via Entropic Optimal Transport</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.08851](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08851) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Jialin Li, Zhuo Zhang, Yue Cao, Guipeng Lan, Jiabao Wen, Shuai Xiao, Jiachen Yang
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Medical Imaging, Optimal Transport, Image Editing
- **Venue**: ICML 2026
- **Summary**: 本文提出了OT-Bridge Editor，将局部编辑重新表述为约束熵最优传输问题，利用几何信息引导生成路径，合成的血管造影图像在公共ARCADE基准和多中心数据集上分别实现了27.8%和23.0%的下游狭窄检测相对提升。

<h3 id="413">[413] BubbleSpec: Turning Long-Tail Bubbles into Speculative Rollout Drafts for Synchronous Reinforcement Learning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.08862](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08862) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yuhang Xu, Kaibin Tian, Yang Tian, Zhice Yang, Yifeng Yu, Yan Li, Shengzhong Liu, Fan Wu, Guihai Chen
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Training, Reinforcement Learning, Speculative Decoding
- **Summary**: 本文提出了BubbleSpec框架，通过利用强化学习rollout阶段长尾气泡中的空闲时间窗口预生成后续步骤的rollout结果作为推测解码草稿，在保持严格同步属性的同时将解码步骤减少50%，rollout吞吐量提升高达1.8倍。

<h3 id="414">[414] Why Do Aligned LLMs Remain Jailbreakable: Refusal-Escape Directions, Operator-Level Sources, and Safety-Utility Trade-off</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.08878](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08878) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yu Chen, Yuanhao Liu, Qi Cao
- **Subjects**: cs.CR; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Security, LLM Alignment, Jailbreak Attack
- **Summary**: 本文从连续输入变换视角研究了为什么对齐的LLM仍然可以被越狱攻击，发现了拒绝-逃逸方向(RED)的存在，并证明了RED可以精确分解为模型算子级源的贡献，揭示了条件性安全-效用权衡的存在。

<h3 id="415">[415] Fitting Is Not Enough: Smoothness in Extremely Quantized LLMs</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.08894](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08894) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yuzhuang Xu, Xu Han, Yuxuan Li, Pengzhan Li, Wanxiang Che
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Model Compression, Quantization
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/xuyuzhuang11/FINE)
- **Summary**: 本文发现极低比特量化的大语言模型除了数值精度损失外还存在系统性的平滑性退化问题，量化模型在预测邻域内有效token候选快速减少导致解码树稀疏和生成质量下降，提出了在量化中保持平滑性的设计原则。

<h3 id="416">[416] FragileFlow: Spectral Control of Correct-but-Fragile Predictions for Foundation Model Robustness</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.08896](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08896) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Zhuoyun Li, Boxuan Wang, Jinwei Hu, Xiaowei Huang, Yi Dong
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Adversarial Robustness, Foundation Model
- **Summary**: 本文提出了FragileFlow，一个插件式正则化器，通过校准边际缓冲区识别正确但脆弱的预测，并将其类别外概率质量组织成类级脆弱风险矩阵，在多项选择LLM基准和少样本CLIP适应任务上持续改善了理论相关的风险度量。

<h3 id="417">[417] Shapley Regression for Rare Disease Diagnosis Support: a case study on APDS</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.08897](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08897) (cross-listed)

- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Medical AI, Interpretability
- **Venue**: IJCAI 2026
- **Summary**: 本文提出Shapley回归方法，用于罕见遗传免疫疾病APDS的诊断支持。该方法用k-加性合作博弈替代线性预测器，显式建模症状共现，在保持逻辑回归透明性的同时提高了预测能力。

<h3 id="418">[418] LLM-Agnostic Semantic Representation Attack</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.08898](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08898) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Jiawei Lian, Jianhong Pan, Lefan Wang, Yi Wang, Tairan Huang, Shaohui Mei, Lap-Pui Chau

- **Tags**: LLM Security, Jailbreak Attack
- **Summary**: 本文提出语义表示攻击(SRA)，一种新的LLM无关攻击范式，将对抗目标从精确文本匹配重新定义为恶意语义表示。该方法在26个开源LLM上实现99.71%的平均攻击成功率，具有强迁移性和隐蔽性。

<h3 id="419">[419] DAPE: Dynamic Non-uniform Alignment and Progressive Detail Enhancement Techniques for Improving the Performance of Efficient Visual Language Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.08902](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08902) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Mengyuan Tian, Qiyan Zhao, Yanan Wang, Da-Han Wang
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Vision-Language Model, Multimodal Learning
- **Venue**: ICIC 2026
- **Summary**: 本文提出DAPE框架，通过动态跨模态对齐和渐进式细节增强来改进视觉语言模型。该方法使用可学习的匹配函数动态分配图像标签，并在多个基准测试中显著提升下游任务准确性。

<h3 id="420">[420] Transformer autoencoder with local attention for sparse and irregular time series with application on risk estimation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.08914](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08914) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Panteleimon Rodis
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Time Series Forecasting, Anomaly Detection, Energy Management
- **Summary**: 本文提出基于局部注意力的Transformer自编码器框架，用于稀疏和不规则时间序列的风险估计。该方法应用于希腊电力系统的非技术性损失检测，有效捕获不规则序列中的相关模式。

<h3 id="421">[421] Improving Lexical Difficulty Prediction with Context-Aligned Contrastive Learning and Ridge Ensembling</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.08950](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08950) (cross-listed)

- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Education Technology, Contrastive Learning
- **Summary**: 本文提出上下文对齐对比回归方法，用于词汇难度预测。该方法结合Ridge回归集成和两个互补目标，改善了跨语言表示对齐并捕获词汇难度的序数结构。

<h3 id="422">[422] MolWorld: Molecule World Models for Actionable Molecular Optimization</h3>

- **Summary**: 本文提出MolWorld框架，将可操作的分子优化建模为分子转移图的序列扩展。该方法通过世界模型引导优化，在保持结构连通性的同时发现高属性分子。

<h3 id="423">[423] Extrusion Segmentation Strategy to improve CAD Reconstruction from Point Cloud</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.08971](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08971) (cross-listed)

- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI

- **Summary**: 本文提出端到端模型从点云重建CAD模型，并引入挤压分割策略将点云分解为独立挤压体。该策略增加了数据多样性，提高了深度学习模型的泛化能力和鲁棒性。

<h3 id="424">[424] Tracking the Truth: Object-Centric Spatio-Temporal Monitoring for Video Large Language Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.08974](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08974) (cross-listed)

- **Summary**: 本文提出STEMO-Bench基准和STEMO-Track框架，用于评估和改进视频大语言模型的时空监控能力。该方法通过显式构建和推理对象轨迹，显著减少幻觉回答并提高时空推理一致性。

<h3 id="425">[425] Benchmarking Compositional Generalisation for Machine Learning Interatomic Potentials</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.08988](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08988) (cross-listed)

- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.CE

- **Summary**: 本文提出一个基准测试，用于评估机器学习原子间势的组合泛化能力。实验表明，即使使用预训练的基础模型，在分布外示例上的误差仍比分布内示例高一个数量级。

<h3 id="426">[426] CT-IDP: Segmentation-Derived Quantitative Phenotypes for Interpretable Abdominal CT Disease Classification</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.09002](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09002) (cross-listed)

- **Summary**: 本文开发CT-IDP定量表型框架，从腹部CT分割中提取900多个器官描述符用于疾病分类。该方法在多个数据集上优于基于视觉Transformer的基线，同时保持可解释性。

<h3 id="427">[427] Towards Backdoor-Based Ownership Verification for Vision-Language-Action Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.09005](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09005) (cross-listed)

- **Summary**: 本文提出GuardVLA，首个针对视觉-语言-动作模型的后门所有权验证框架。该方法通过在训练中嵌入隐蔽无害的后门水印，实现可靠的模型所有权验证。

<h3 id="428">[428] Large Language Models for Sequential Decision-Making: Improving In-Context Learning via Supervised Fine-Tuning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.09009](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09009) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Minmin Zhang, Sina Aghaei, Soroush Saghafian
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: In-Context Learning, Decision Making, Reinforcement Learning
- **Summary**: 本文研究大语言模型在序贯决策中的上下文学习能力，包括MDP、POMDP和APOMDP设置。通过监督微调，LLM在部分可观察和模型模糊环境中显著优于仅使用上下文的基线。

<h3 id="429">[429] A Geometric Perspective on Next-Token Prediction in Large Language Models: Three Emerging Phases</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.09011](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09011) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Gianfranco Lombardo, Giuseppe Trimigno, Stefano Cagnoni
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Inference, Interpretability, Deep Learning Theory
- **Summary**: 本文从几何视角研究大语言模型中预测信息的演变，识别出三个阶段：播种复用、提升覆盖和焦点收敛。研究发现更深LLM的额外容量主要用于候选消歧。

<h3 id="430">[430] Evolutionary Ensemble of Agents</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.09018](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09018) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Zongmin Yu, Liu Yang
- **Subjects**: cs.NE; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Multi-Agent System, Evolutionary Computation
- **Summary**: 本文提出EvE框架，将现有编码代理组织成协同进化的系统用于算法发现。该方法在ICON研究瓶颈上自主发现了可靠的重新缩放-插值机制，突破了静态性能上限。

<h3 id="431">[431] A Quantum Inspired Variational Kernel and Explainable AI Framework for Cross Region Solar and Wind Energy Forecasting</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.09032](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09032) (cross-listed)

- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Time Series Forecasting, Energy Management, Explainable AI
- **Summary**: 本文提出四阶段混合框架用于跨区域太阳能和风能预测，结合经典基线、量子启发变分核和生成式AI可解释层。该方法在三个区域数据上表现优异，能有效区分平静和风暴天气。

<h3 id="432">[432] ShadowMerge: A Novel Poisoning Attack on Graph-Based Agent Memory via Relation-Channel Conflicts</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.09033](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09033) (cross-listed)

- **Subjects**: cs.CR; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Security, Data Poisoning, LLM Agent
- **Summary**: 本文提出SHADOWMERGE攻击，利用关系通道冲突对基于图的代理记忆进行投毒。该方法在三个真实数据集上实现93.8%的平均攻击成功率，比最佳基线提高50.3个百分点。

<h3 id="433">[433] Phase Transitions in Affective Meaning Divergence: The Hidden Drift Before the Break</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.09043](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09043) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Napassorn Litchiowong
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Multi-Agent System, LLM Agent, Fault Tolerance
- **Summary**: 本文提出Self-Anchored Consensus(SAC)协议，一种完全去中心化的迭代过滤精化协议，使LLM多智能体系统能够在拜占庭故障下保持鲁棒性。实验证明SAC有效抑制拜占庭影响，在多种通信拓扑上持续提升性能。

<h3 id="434">[434] Octopus Protocol: One-Shot Hardware Discovery and Control for AI Agents via Infrastructure-as-Prompts</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.09055](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09055) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Quilee Simeon, Justin M. Wei, Yile Fan
- **Subjects**: cs.RO; cs.AI; cs.MA
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Robotics, Tool Learning
- **Summary**: 本文提出Octopus Protocol系统，通过五阶段流水线使AI代理能够自动发现和控制硬件设备，生成MCP服务器而无需人工编写代码。系统在多个平台和机械臂上验证，实现了闭环视觉运动控制。

<h3 id="435">[435] Single-Configuration Attack Success Rate Is Not Enough: Jailbreak Evaluations Should Report Distributional Attack Success</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.09070](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09070) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Carsten Maple, Abhishek Kumar, Riya Tapwal
- **Subjects**: cs.CR; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Anomaly Detection, Industrial AI, Dataset
- **Summary**: 本文介绍FactoryNet，首个工业时序数据通用预训练语料库，包含5100万数据点和2.3万次任务执行。数据集采用S-E-F-C模式，支持跨实体迁移和异常检测，为工业基础模型研究奠定基础。

<h3 id="436">[436] Robust Multi-Agent LLMs under Byzantine Faults</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.09076](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09076) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Haejoon Lee, Vincent-Daniel Yun, Hyeonho Oh, Dimitra Panagou, Sai Praneeth Karimireddy
- **Subjects**: cs.MA; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Multi-Agent System, LLM Agent, Fault Tolerance
- **Summary**: 本文研究去中心化LLM多智能体系统，提出了一种完全去中心化的迭代过滤与精炼协议——自锚定共识（SAC），使智能体能够在拜占庭故障影响下保持和传播可靠信息。实验表明，SAC能有效抑制拜占庭影响，在数学和常识推理基准测试中持续提升性能。

<h3 id="437">[437] FactoryNet: A Large-Scale Dataset toward Industrial Time-Series Foundation Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.09081](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09081) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Karim Othman, Jonas Petersen, Matei Ignuta-Ciuncanu, Riccardo Maggioni, Camilla Mazzoleni, Federico Martelli, Philipp Petersen
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Anomaly Detection, Foundation Model, Industrial AI
- **Summary**: 本文介绍了首个工业时序数据通用预训练语料库FactoryNet，包含5100万个数据点，涵盖六种实体的23000次任务执行。该数据集采用新颖的S-E-F-C模式，支持零样本跨实体迁移和高效的异常检测。

<h3 id="438">[438] Field-Localized Forgery Detection for Digital Identity Documents</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.09089](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09089) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Abhishek Kumar, Riya Tapwal, Carsten Maple, Mark Hooper
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Image Forensics, Document Understanding, Biometric Security
- **Summary**: 本文提出FLiD框架，通过定位关键身份区域而非处理完整文档图像来检测数字身份证件的局部篡改。该方法在人脸和文本字段攻击检测上显著优于全文档基线，同时参数量和计算量大幅降低。

<h3 id="439">[439] Investigating Anisotropy in Visual Grounding under Controlled Counterfactual Perturbations</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.09090](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09090) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Gabriele Lombardo, Luigi Maiorana, Liliana Lo Presti, Marco La Cascia
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Visual Grounding, Interpretability
- **Venue**: CVPR 2026 Workshop
- **Summary**: 本文研究视觉定位模型在语义不匹配标题下的近似行为，通过受控反事实扰动实验检验嵌入各向异性是否导致反事实失败。结果表明各向异性本身不能解释反事实错误，鲁棒性需要研究更细粒度的嵌入空间几何特性。

<h3 id="440">[440] Contextual Plackett-Luce: An Efficient Neural Model for Probabilistic Sequence Selection under Ambiguity</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.09112](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09112) (cross-listed)

- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Trajectory Prediction, Probabilistic Inference
- **Summary**: 本文提出Contextual Plackett-Luce(CPL)模型，用于模糊监督下的序列选择问题。CPL结合并行评分和轻量级自回归选择，在多模态路径预测和代表性子集选择任务上表现出更好的结构一致性和鲁棒性。

<h3 id="441">[441] AI Native Asset Intelligence</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.09115](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09115) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Gal Engelberg, Leon Goldberg, Konstantin Koutsyi, Boris Plotnikov, Tiltan Gilat, Ben Benhemo
- **Subjects**: cs.CR; cs.AI

- **Summary**: 本文提出AI原生资产智能框架，将异构安全数据转化为结构化智能层，实现一致的、上下文相关的资产级推理。框架结合建模层和评分层，在包含131,625个资源的生产快照上验证了评分系统的有效性。

<h3 id="442">[442] Personalized Alignment Revisited: The Necessity and Sufficiency of User Diversity</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.09119](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09119) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Enoch Hyunwook Kang
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: LLM Alignment, LLM Personalization
- **Summary**: 本文刻画了个性化对齐实现最优在线遗憾和离线样本复杂度的理论条件，证明用户多样性条件——用户特定头部群体必须跨越能够改变最优响应的潜在奖励方向——既是必要也是充分的。

<h3 id="443">[443] A Communication-Theoretic Framework for LLM Agents: Cost-Aware Adaptive Reliability</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.09121](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09121) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Hamed Omidvar, Vahideh Akhlaghi
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.CL; cs.IT

- **Summary**: 本文将LLM代理框架化为离散随机信道，统一了重试、投票、自一致性等可靠性技术。作者推导了噪声方差阈值和生成器-评论器精化的收缩性准则，并引入成本感知路由器在质量-成本前沿上取得帕累托最优。

<h3 id="444">[444] Internal vs. External: Comparing Deliberation and Evolution for Multi-Agent Constitutional Design</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.09128](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09128) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Hershraj Niranjani, Ujwal Kumar, Phan Xuan Tan
- **Subjects**: cs.MA; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Multi-Agent System, Evolutionary Computation, Social Simulation
- **Summary**: 本文首次对比研究了内部协商和外部进化两种多智能体系统行为宪法设计方法。实验表明，在集体行动场景中进化显著优于协商，但在双边交易中两种方法均无改善；进化可靠地发现惩罚机制，而协商从未提出。

<h3 id="445">[445] From Traditional Taggers to LLMs: A Comparative Study of POS Tagging for Medieval Romance Languages</h3>

- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; stat.AP
- **Tags**: Low-Resource NLP, Transfer Learning, Part-of-Speech Tagging

- **Summary**: 本文系统评估了LLM在中世纪罗曼语（奥克语、加泰罗尼亚语、法语）词性标注任务上的表现，比较了传统标注器与LLM在零样本、少样本、微调和跨语言迁移设置下的效果。结果表明LLM方法显著优于传统方法，跨语言迁移对低资源变体尤其有益。

<h3 id="446">[446] Beyond Self-Play: Hierarchical Reasoning for Continuous Motion in Closed-Loop Traffic Simulation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.09153](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09153) (cross-listed)

<h3 id="447">[447] Lost in Translation? Exploring the Shift in Grammatical Gender from Latin to Occitan</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.09156](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09156) (cross-listed)

- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Low-Resource NLP, Transfer Learning, Historical Linguistics
- **Venue**: ACL 2026 Workshop
- **Summary**: 本文使用可解释深度学习框架研究拉丁语到罗曼语语法性别系统的演变，分析了形态学特征和词性类别对性别预测的贡献。作者提出了适用于低资源历史语言的改进分词器，并刻画了性别信息在词元和句子语境间的分布。

<h3 id="448">[448] Revisiting Mixture Policies in Entropy-Regularized Actor-Critic</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.09157](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09157) (cross-listed)

- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI

- **Summary**: 本文研究了连续动作强化学习中的混合策略，提出边缘化重参数化估计器以降低方差。实验表明，混合策略在多个基准上可达到或超越高斯策略的性能，将混合策略从理论好奇转变为实用工具。

<h3 id="449">[449] WorldSpeech: A Multilingual Speech Corpus from Around the World</h3>

- **Summary**: 本文介绍了WorldSpeech，一个涵盖76种语言、总计6.5万小时对齐音频-文本数据的24kHz多语言语音语料库。在11种类型多样的语言上微调现有ASR模型，平均相对词错误率降低了63.5%。

<h3 id="450">[450] Prediction Bottlenecks Don't Discover Causal Structure (But Here's What They Actually Do)</h3>

- **Tags**: Causal Inference, Benchmark, Time Series Analysis
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/ankitlade12/ssm-causal)
- **Summary**: 本文对预测瓶颈能够发现因果结构的声称进行了系统性证伪，发现该方法在标准化基准测试中不如经典方法（如Lasso和PCMCI），且所谓的干预优势主要是样本量混淆。作者将测试协议打包为可复用的证伪基准。

<h3 id="451">[451] WavesFM: Hierarchical Representation Learning for Longitudinal Wearable Sensor Waveforms</h3>

- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Foundation Model, Wearable Computing, Self-Supervised Learning, Healthcare Monitoring
- **Summary**: 本文提出WavesFM，一个用于纵向生理数据的基础模型，采用两阶段自监督学习框架：首先预训练片段级编码器提取局部嵌入，然后训练时序编码器建模多日跨度的嵌入序列。该模型在超过680万小时数据上预训练，在58个多样化任务上展现优越性能。

<h3 id="452">[452] Navigating LLM Valley: From AdamW to Memory-Efficient and Matrix-Based Optimizers</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.09176](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09176) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Aditya Ranganath
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Optimization, LLM Training, Survey
- **Summary**: 本综述从系统和优化视角回顾了大语言模型的优化器设计，将文献组织为经典一阶优化器、自适应优化器、内存高效变体、二阶方法、符号优化器和矩阵优化器等类别。作者认为LLM优化器研究正进入新阶段，需要更严格的规模感知比较。

<h3 id="453">[453] DARE: Difficulty-Adaptive Reinforcement Learning with Co-Evolved Difficulty Estimation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.09188](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09188) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yang Zhou, Can Jin, Zihan Dong, Zhepeng Wang, Yanting Yang, Shiyu Zhao, Lei Li, Runxue Bao, Yaochen Xie, Dimitris N. Metaxas
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Reinforcement Learning, LLM Reasoning, Curriculum Learning
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/EtaYang10th/DARE)
- **Summary**: 本文提出DARE框架，通过自归一化重要性采样将难度估计与策略协同演化，在对称Beta采样分布下保持多样化难度覆盖，并针对不同难度层级应用定制训练策略。实验表明DARE在训练效率、最终效果和推理效率上均优于现有方法。

<h3 id="454">[454] RigidFormer: Learning Rigid Dynamics using Transformers</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.09196](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09196) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Zhiyang Dou, Minghao Guo, Haixu Wu, Doug Roble, Tuur Stuyck, Wojciech Matusik
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; cs.GR; cs.LG; cs.RO
- **Tags**: Physics Simulation, 3D Vision, Transformer Architecture
- **Summary**: 本文提出RigidFormer，一个基于Transformer的物体中心模型，可从无网格表示（如点云）学习刚体动力学。该模型通过锚点-顶点池化保留接触相关几何信息，使用基于锚点的RoPE注入几何信息，并通过可微分Kabsch对齐强制刚体约束。

<h3 id="455">[455] Select-then-differentiate: Solving Bilevel Optimization with Manifold Lower-level Solution Sets</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.09209](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09209) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Saeed Masiha, Zebang Shen, Negar Kiyavash, Niao He
- **Subjects**: math.OC; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Optimization, LLM Training, Mathematical Reasoning
- **Summary**: 本文研究下层问题具有非孤立极小值流形的乐观双层优化，提出HG-MS方法结合显式乐观选择与高效的伪逆超梯度计算。该方法在LLM源重加权任务上取得了最佳的GSM8K/MATH分数和有竞争力的MT-Bench指令遵循结果。

<h3 id="456">[456] Fast Rates for Offline Contextual Bandits with Forward-KL Regularization under Single-Policy Concentrability</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.09214](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09214) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Qingyue Zhao, Kaixuan Ji, Heyang Zhao, Quanquan Gu
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.IT; math.ST; stat.ML
- **Tags**: Reinforcement Learning, Optimization, Distributional RL
- **Summary**: 本文首次为前向KL正则化的离线上下文赌博机问题提供了O(ε^-1)快速率上界，在表格和一般函数逼近设置下均成立。作者还提供了速率最优的下界，证明了上界的紧致性。

<h3 id="457">[457] Flame3D: Zero-shot Compositional Reasoning of 3D Scenes with Agentic Language Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.09218](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09218) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Sagar Bharadwaj, Ziyong Ma, Anurag Ghosh, Srinivasan Seshan, Anthony Rowe
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; cs.LG; cs.RO
- **Tags**: 3D Vision, Vision-Language Model, LLM Agent
- **Summary**: 本文提出Flame3D，一个无需训练的3D场景理解框架，将场景表示为可编辑的视觉-文本3D记忆，并通过可组合的空间工具暴露给现成的MLLM。该框架在ScanQA上与微调方法性能相当，并在新构建的Compose3D基准上展示了多跳3D推理能力。

<h3 id="458">[458] The Pokémon Theorem and other Fairness Impossibility Results</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.09221](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09221) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Daniel Matsui Smola, Alex Smola
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Fairness, AI Ethics, Representation Learning
- **Summary**: 本文通过RKHS几何视角统一了多个公平性不可能结果，表明公平性标准是条件均值嵌入上的线性约束，而不平等的基础率使这些约束被过度确定。作者推导出新的不可能结果，包括"宝可梦定理"和公平特征学习的不可能性。

<h3 id="459">[459] Detect, Localize, and Explain: Interactive Hierarchical Log Anomaly Analytics with LLM Augmentation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.09222](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09222) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Lei Ma, Suhani Chaudhary, Ethan Shanbaum, Athanasios Tassiadamis, Peter M. VanNostrand, Dennis M. Hofmann, Haowen Xu, Elke Rundensteiner
- **Subjects**: cs.DB; cs.AI; cs.SE
- **Tags**: Log Analysis, Anomaly Detection, LLM Application, Explainable AI
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/LeiMa0324/KRONE_Demo_official)
- **Summary**: 本文介绍Krone-viz，一个交互式可视化系统，将扁平日志序列转换为语义连贯的层次执行单元，并执行模块化异常检测。系统支持检查层次分解、查看带有LLM解释的检测结果，以及人在回路的知识修订。

<h3 id="460">[460] The Art of the Jailbreak: Formulating Jailbreak Attacks for LLM Security Beyond Binary Scoring</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.09225](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09225) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Ismail Hossain, Tanzim Ahad, Md Jahangir Alam, Sai Puppala, Syed Bahauddin Alam, Sajedul Talukder
- **Subjects**: cs.CR; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Jailbreak Attack, LLM Security, Red Teaming
- **Summary**: 本文针对LLM越狱攻击缺乏系统性研究基础设施的问题，构建了包含114,000个对抗性提示的大规模数据集，开发了自动生成越狱提示的模型，并提出了OPTIMUS评估器，通过连续指标超越传统二元评估的局限性。

<h3 id="461">[461] ProactBench: Beyond What The User Asked For</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.09228](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09228) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Sepehr Harfi, Ahmad Salimi, Dongming Shen, Alex Smola
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Evaluation, Benchmark, Dialogue System
- **Summary**: 本文引入ProactBench基准，用于评估LLM的对话主动性，将其分解为涌现型、关键型和恢复型三种类型。基准包含198个精选对话和624个触发点，发现恢复型主动性既困难又难以被标准基准预测。

<h3 id="462">[462] Towards Robust Sequential Decomposition for Complex Image Editing</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.09233](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09233) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Zilai Zeng, Mingdeng Cao, Zijie Li, Xiaochen Lian, Yichun Shi, Peihao Zhu, Chen Sun, Peng Wang
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Image Editing, Diffusion Model, Text-to-Image
- **Venue**: CVPR 2026
- **Summary**: 本文研究复杂图像编辑的序列分解方法，开发了合成数据流水线构建不同复杂度的编辑任务。通过在合成数据上微调，发现适当设计的序列分解范式在任务复杂度增加时仍能获得鲁棒改进，且分解技能可迁移到真实图像。

<h3 id="463">[463] On Variance Reduction in Learning Mean Flows</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.09235](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09235) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Juanwu Lu, Ziran Wang
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; stat.ML
- **Tags**: Diffusion Model, Flow Matching, Generative Model
- **Summary**: 本文建立了MeanFlow训练不稳定性理论，将其归因于条件速度场的错误使用，并推导出最优系数的闭式解。最优系数在二维基准上带来高达54%的样本质量改进，同时揭示了FID-MSE景观不匹配现象。

<h3 id="464">[464] Matching Meaning at Scale: Evaluating Semantic Search for 18th-Century Intellectual History through the Case of Locke</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.09236](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09236) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yu Wu, Ananth Mahadevan, Filip Ginter, Michael Mathioudakis, Mikko Tolonen
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.CY; cs.DL; cs.IR
- **Tags**: Semantic Search, Information Retrieval, Digital Humanities
- **Venue**: NLP4DH 2026
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/COMHIS/locke-sim-data)
- **Summary**: 本文通过洛克著作的接受史案例，评估语义搜索在18世纪思想史研究中的效果。结果表明语义搜索比词汇基线检索到更多隐含接受，但也存在"词汇把关"效应，即检索仍部分受限于表面词汇重叠。

<h3 id="465">[465] Intrinsic Muon: Spectral Optimization on Riemannian Matrix Manifolds</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.09238](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09238) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yibang Li, Bihari Lal Pandey, Ravi Sah, Andi Han, Cyrus Mostajeran, Pratik Jawanpuria, Bamdev Mishra
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Optimization, Transfer Learning, Representation Learning
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/1bang118/manifold-intrinsic-muon)
- **Summary**: 本文提出了内在Muon (iMuon)框架，将Muon优化器扩展到黎曼矩阵流形上，在固定秩、SPD、Stiefel和Grassmann流形上实现闭式更新，并在LoRA微调、图像分类和子空间学习任务中验证了其有效性。

<h3 id="466">[466] Sub-JEPA: Subspace Gaussian Regularization for Stable End-to-End World Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.09241](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09241) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Kai Zhao, Dongliang Nie, Yuchen Lin, Zhehan Luo, Yixiao Gu, Deng-Ping Fan, Dan Zeng
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: World Model, Representation Learning, Self-Supervised Learning
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/intcomp/Sub-JEPA)
- **Summary**: 本文提出了Sub-JEPA方法，通过在多个随机子空间而非原始嵌入空间中应用高斯约束，在训练稳定性和表示质量之间取得更好的平衡，在四个连续控制环境中显著优于LeWM基线。

<h3 id="467">[467] Cornerstones or Stumbling Blocks? Deciphering the Rock Tokens in On-Policy Distillation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.09253](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09253) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yuxuan Jiang, Runchao Li, Shubhashis Roy Dipta, Dawei Li, Zhao Yang
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Knowledge Distillation, LLM Reasoning
- **Summary**: 本文发现在策略蒸馏训练中存在持续高损失的"岩石令牌"，这些令牌消耗大量优化带宽但对推理性能贡献甚微，表明可以通过策略性绕过这些令牌来提高蒸馏效率。

<h3 id="468">[468] Improving Generalization by Permutation Routing Across Model Copies</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.09256](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09256) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Shuhei Kashiwamura, Timothee Leleu
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; stat.ML
- **Tags**: Generalization Bound, Optimization
- **Summary**: 本文引入M覆盖变换方法，通过结构化置换混合核在模型副本间路由计算路径，而非参数平均，从而通过结构化消息共享而非副本坍缩来改善泛化性能。

<h3 id="469">[469] Monocular Biomechanical Tracking of Fingers with Inverse Kinematics to Foundation Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.09258](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09258) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: R. James Cotton, Pouyan Firouzabadi, Wendy Murray
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Foundation Model, Pose Estimation, Medical AI
- **Venue**: EMBC 2026
- **Summary**: 本文提出了一种将SAM 3D Body基础模型与逆运动学优化相结合的方法，从单目视频中提取解剖约束的手指关节角度，在多视角重建验证中达到约10度的关节角度误差和约6毫米的手部位置误差。

<h3 id="470">[470] Remix the Timbre: Diffusion-Based Style Transfer Across Polyphonic Stems</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.09259](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09259) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Leduo Chen, Junchuan Zhao, Shengchen Li
- **Subjects**: cs.SD; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Diffusion Model, Music Generation, Audio Processing
- **Summary**: 本文提出了MixtureTT系统，这是首个直接从复调混合音频进行灵活逐轨音色迁移的方法，通过联合轨扩散变换器消除级联分离误差，并将推理成本降低为轨数分之一。

<h3 id="471">[471] Beyond Continuity: Challenges of Context Switching in Multi-Turn Dialogue with LLMs</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.09268](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09268) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Aditya Sinha, Harald Steck, Vito Ostuni, Matteo Rinaldi
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Multi-Turn Interaction, Dialogue System, LLM Evaluation
- **Venue**: ICLR 2026 Workshop
- **Summary**: 本文研究了LLM在多轮对话中处理上下文切换的能力，发现模型经常忽略话题转换并携带无关上下文。作者构建了合成基准来评估话题转向检测和相关上下文筛选任务，测试了10个LLM后发现只有推理型和强指令型模型能准确检测转向，所有模型都存在位置偏差。

<h3 id="472">[472] Memorize Theorems, Not Instances: Probing SFT Generalization through Mathematical Reasoning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.09270](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09270) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Ruiying Peng, Mengyu Yang, Jing Lei, Xiaohui Li, Xueyu Wu, Xinlei Chen
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Dialogue System, LLM Evaluation, Long Context
- **Venue**: ICLR 2026 Workshop
- **Summary**: 本文评估了LLM在多轮对话中检测话题转换和筛选相关上下文的能力，发现只有推理型和强指令型LLM能准确检测转换，开源模型表现较差，且所有模型都存在位置偏差。

<h3 id="473">[473] Semi-Supervised Neural Super-Resolution for Mesh-Based Simulations</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.09284](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09284) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Jiyeon Kim, Youngjoon Hong, Won-Yong Shin
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.CE
- **Tags**: Mathematical Reasoning, Instruction Tuning, LLM Reasoning
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/jykim-git/SuperMeshNet.git)
- **Summary**: 本文提出Theorem-SFT方法，将监督目标从记忆问题-答案对转向学习定理应用规则，在MATH基准上提升8.8%，在GeoQA上提升20.27%，表明泛化失败源于记忆了错误的归纳目标。

<h3 id="474">[474] MC$^2$: Monte Carlo Correction for Fast Elliptic PDE Solving</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.09288](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09288) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Ethan Hsu, Hong Meng Yam, Ivan Ge
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.CE; cs.CV; math.NA
- **Tags**: Scientific Computing, Neural Operator, Benchmark
- **Summary**: 本文提出MC²，一种混合蒙特卡洛-神经网络PDE求解器，通过学习神经网络修正来从低预算蒙特卡洛解中恢复高保真解。该方法比纯蒙特卡洛快约1000倍，同时作者发布了PDEZoo，这是迄今最大的标准化椭圆PDE基准，包含200万个PDE。

<h3 id="475">[475] Towards Effective Theory of LLMs: A Representation Learning Approach</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.09294](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09294) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Muhammed Ustaomeroglu, Guannan Qu
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Representation Learning, Interpretability, LLM Reasoning
- **Summary**: 本文提出表征有效理论(RET)框架，通过自监督学习从LLM隐藏状态轨迹中学习宏观状态。RET能够揭示推理的心理状态轨迹、捕获高层语义结构、早期预测行为结果（如迎合行为），并提供因果控制手段来引导生成走向可解释的计算阶段。

<h3 id="476">[476] Micro-Defects Expose Macro-Fakes: Detecting AI-Generated Images via Local Distributional Shifts</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.09296](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09296) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Boxuan Zhang, Jianing Zhu, Qifan Wang, Jiang Liu, Ruixiang Tang
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Deepfake Detection, AI-Generated Image, Image Quality Assessment
- **Summary**: 本文提出了MDMF框架，通过学习补丁取证签名和最大均值差异来放大微观统计不规则性，从而检测AI生成图像，在多个基准上持续优于基线检测器。

<h3 id="477">[477] Neural Cluster First, Route Second: One-Shot Capacitated Vehicle Routing via Differentiable Optimal Transport</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.09301](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09301) (cross-listed)

- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI

- **Summary**: 本文提出了Neural CFRS框架，这是首个纯非自回归单次神经容量车辆路径问题求解器，通过可微熵最优传输层实现端到端的全局车队容量约束，在分布外扩展时保持稳健性能。

<h3 id="478">[478] Hierarchical Attention-based Graph Neural Network with Relevance-driven Pruning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.09308](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09308) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Seungwoo Kum
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Graph Neural Network, Model Compression, Interpretability
- **Summary**: 本文提出了HA-HeteroGNN框架，通过双层注意力机制产生节点相关性分数用于剪枝，在减少27%图边的同时提高分类准确率2.4-6.1%，挑战了剪枝必然牺牲准确率的传统假设。

<h3 id="479">[479] Teaching Molecular Dynamics to a Non-Autoregressive Ionic Transport Predictor</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.09311](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09311) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Jiyeon Kim, Byungju Lee, Won-Yong Shin
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Molecular Dynamics, Material Discovery, Scientific Computing
- **Venue**: ICML 2026
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/jykim-git/MD)
- **Summary**: 本文提出了一种基于辅助模态学习的非自回归框架，在训练时利用原子轨迹但不需推理时输入，相比自回归模型实现200倍以上加速，同时显著降低预测误差。

<h3 id="480">[480] Neural Information Causality</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.09316](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09316) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Jeongho Bang, Marcin Pawłowski
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Representation Learning, Information Theory, Deep Learning Theory
- **Summary**: 本文将信息因果性嵌入表示学习，提出了神经信息因果性框架，为查询分离架构提供了查询泄漏、精度泄漏和情节特定记忆的操作性诊断工具。

<h3 id="481">[481] The Trap of Trajectory: Towards Understanding and Mitigating Spurious Correlations in Agentic Memory</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.09330](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09330) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Luoxi Tang, Rupali Rajendra Vaje, Yuqiao Meng, Sakshi Sunil Narkar, Weicheng Ma, Zeyu Ding, Dazheng Zhang, Zhaohan Xi
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, LLM Reasoning, Memory Architecture
- **Summary**: 本文研究了智能体记忆系统中的虚假相关性问题，即检索到的记忆可能携带错误关联证据并在下游决策中传播错误推理。作者提出了CAMEL方法，一种即插即用的校准方法，能够在多种记忆架构中减少对虚假模式的依赖，同时保持或提高在干净输入上的性能。

<h3 id="482">[482] Perceptual Asymmetry Between Hue Categories: Evidence from Human Color Categorization</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.09339](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09339) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Elnara Kadyrgali, Nuray Toganas, Muragul Muratbekova, Pakizar Shamoi
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Cognitive Science, Computer Vision
- **Summary**: 本文通过大规模人类颜色分类数据分析色调类别间的感知不对称性，发现黄色占据紧凑锐利的区域，而绿色跨越更宽的区间并具有更长的过渡结构。研究结果表明感知颜色类别不仅模糊，而且在几何组织上高度不均匀，为COLIBRI模糊颜色模型提供了新的解释视角。

<h3 id="483">[483] RuPLaR : Efficient Latent Compression of LLM Reasoning Chains with Rule-Based Priors From Multi-Step to One-Step</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.09346](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09346) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Xiaocheng Luo, Kang Wang, Zaifu Zhan, Yuechi Zhou, Xiangyu Duan
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Code Generation, Scientific Computing, LLM Evaluation
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/xiaocen-luo/RuPLaR)
- **Summary**: 本文解决了LLM生成科学模拟代码中的理解-生成差距问题，即代码可以成功执行但编码了错误的物理方程。作者提出了意图保真度分数(IFS)和基于PDE的细化循环，用于验证生成代码与预期数学结构的一致性。

<h3 id="484">[484] HOME-KGQA: A Benchmark Dataset for Multimodal Knowledge Graph Question Answering on Household Daily Activities</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.09348](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09348) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Shusaku Egami, Aoi Ohta, Tomoki Tsujimura, Masaki Asada, Tatsuya Ishigaki, Ken Fukuda, Masahiro Hamasaki, Hiroya Takamura
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.DB; cs.MM
- **Tags**: Question Answering, Knowledge Graph, Multimodal Learning, Benchmark
- **Venue**: LREC 2026
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/aistairc/home-kgqa)
- **Summary**: 本文介绍了HOME-KGQA，一个基于家庭日常活动多模态知识图谱的问答基准数据集，包含涉及多级时空推理、多模态接地和聚合函数的复杂多跳问题。实验表明现有LLM-based KGQA方法在该基准上表现不佳，突显了KGQA系统实际部署面临的挑战。

<h3 id="485">[485] Skill-R1: Agent Skill Evolution via Reinforcement Learning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.09359](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09359) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yash Vishe, Rohan Surana, Xunyi Jiang, Zihan Huang, Xintong Li, Nikki Lijing Kuang, Tong Yu, Ryan A. Rossi, Jingbo Shang, Julian McAuley, Junda Wu
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Reinforcement Learning, Skill Learning
- **Summary**: 本文提出了Skill-R1强化学习框架，用于从可验证奖励中进行实例级循环技能优化，训练轻量级技能生成器为冻结的任务LLM生成技能。该方法通过双层组相对策略优化目标实现跨代技能进化，在复杂多步任务上显著优于无技能基线和标准GRPO。

<h3 id="486">[486] Your Simulation Runs but Solves the Wrong Physics: PDE-Grounded Intent Verification for LLM-Generated Multiphysics Simulation Code</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.09360](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09360) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Zhenghan Song, Yulong Liu, Cheng Wan, Chenjun Li, Lingfu Liu, Yunyi Li, Congcong Yuan
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.CL; cs.SE
- **Tags**: Code Generation, Scientific Computing, Program Verification
- **Summary**: 本文指出LLM生成的科学模拟代码存在"理解-生成差距"问题，即代码可以成功执行但编码了错误的物理方程。作者提出了意图保真度分数(IFS)来度量生成代码与预期物理方程的结构匹配程度，并开发了基于PDE的迭代修正循环。在MOOSE框架和新建的MooseBench基准测试上验证了方法有效性，揭示了代码可执行性与意图保真度是两种独立的失败模式。

<h3 id="487">[487] From Detection to Recovery: Operational Analysis on LLM Pre-training with 504 GPUs</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.09370](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09370) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Daemyung Kang, Eunjin Hwang, Hanjeong Lee, HyeokJin Kim, Hyunhoi Koo, Jeongkyu Shin, Jeongseok Kang, Jihyun Kang, Joongi Kim, Junbum Lee, Jungseung Yang, Kyujin Cho, Youngsook Song
- **Subjects**: cs.DC; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Training, Distributed Training, GPU Computing
- **Summary**: 本文对63节点NVIDIA B200生产集群(504 GPU)进行了实证分析，使用55天的Prometheus时间序列数据和73天的操作日志，研究了GPU故障检测、检查点事件和多节点故障响应。研究发现自动重试链成功率是手动恢复的2.7倍，为大规模分布式训练提供了运维洞察。

<h3 id="488">[488] EduStory: A Unified Framework for Pedagogically-Consistent Multi-Shot STEM Instructional Video Generation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.09378](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09378) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Xinyi Wu, Jayant Teotia, Shuai Zhao, Erik Cambria
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: Video Generation, Education Technology, Benchmark
- **Summary**: 本文提出了EduStory框架，用于生成教学一致的多镜头STEM教学视频，集成了教学状态建模、脚本引导的结构化控制和学习导向的评估指标。作者还引入了EduVideoBench诊断基准，包含教学故事板、镜头级语义和知识状态转换的多粒度标注。

<h3 id="489">[489] LiteMedCoT-VL: Parameter-Efficient Adaptation for Medical Visual Question Answering</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.09384](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09384) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Runze Ma, Shunbo Jia, Haonan Lyu, Guo Liu, Caizhi Liao
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; q-bio.QM
- **Tags**: Medical AI, Vision-Language Model, Knowledge Distillation, Question Answering
- **Summary**: 本文提出了LiteMedCoT-VL管道，通过基于LoRA的微调将235B教师模型的思维链推理迁移到2B学生模型，用于医学视觉问答。该方法在PMC-VQA基准上达到64.9%准确率，超过零样本Qwen3-VL-4B基线11个百分点，证明带推理蒸馏的2B模型可匹敌两倍参数的模型。

<h3 id="490">[490] Kinetic-Optimal Scheduling with Moment Correction for Metric-Induced Discrete Flow Matching in Zero-Shot Text-to-Speech</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.09386](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09386) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Dong Yang, Yiyi Cai, Haoyu Zhang, Yuki Saito, Hiroshi Saruwatari
- **Subjects**: eess.AS; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Speech Synthesis, Flow Matching
- **Summary**: 本文针对度量诱导离散流匹配(MI-DFM)提出了动力学最优调度器和有限步矩校正方法，应用于零样本文本转语音。GibbsTTS方法在客观自然度上达到最佳，主观评估中优于掩码离散生成基线，并在说话人相似度上表现强劲。

<h3 id="491">[491] Sparsity Moves Computation: How FFN Architecture Reshapes Attention in Small Transformers</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.09403](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09403) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Gabriel Smithline, Chris Mascioli
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.NE
- **Tags**: Transformer Architecture, Mixture-of-Experts, Deep Learning Theory
- **Summary**: 本文研究了Transformer前馈网络(FFN)架构选择如何重塑模型其他部分的计算，比较了密集FFN、GLU、MoE和MoE-GLU。研究发现稀疏MoE路由可将计算从FFN转移到注意力层，GLU式门控将任务相关的傅里叶结构旋转到分布式子空间，表明局部FFN设计选择具有非局部影响。

<h3 id="492">[492] RePO-VLA: Recovery-Driven Policy Optimization for Vision-Language-Action Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.09410](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09410) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Weijia Liufu, Xiaoyu Guo, Ruiyi Chen, Jingzhi Liu, Kaidong Zhang, Xiwen Liang, Jianqi Lin, Dawei Sun, Yuze Wang, Rongtao Xu, Bingqian Lin, Bowen Yang, Tongtong Cao, Bowen Peng, Dongyu Zhang, Guangrun Wang, Min Wang, Liang Lin, Xiaodan Liang
- **Subjects**: cs.RO; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Vision-Language Model, Robotics, Reinforcement Learning
- **Summary**: 本文提出了RePO-VLA恢复驱动策略优化框架，为视觉-语言-动作模型分配不同角色给成功、恢复和失败轨迹。该方法通过恢复感知初始化、进度感知语义价值函数和价值条件细化，在模拟和真实世界双臂任务中将对抗成功率从20%提升至75%。

<h3 id="493">[493] Relational Retrieval: Leveraging Known-Novel Interactions for Generalized Category Discovery</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.09420](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09420) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yulin Xu, Chunqi Guo, Yuanzhen Shuai, Jianyuan Ni
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; cs.MM
- **Tags**: Semi-Supervised Learning, Open-World Learning
- **Venue**: ICMR 2026
- **Summary**: 本文从关系检索角度解决广义类别发现问题，提出了关系模式一致性(RPC)方法，通过双向知识转移实现标记和未标记数据的相互增强。该方法利用样本与已知类原型保持不变关系的特性，将不可靠的伪标签转化为明确定义的关系模式匹配，在通用和细粒度基准上达到最优性能。

<h3 id="494">[494] AtteConDA: Attention-Based Conflict Suppression in Multi-Condition Diffusion Models and Synthetic Data Augmentation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.09425](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09425) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Shogo Noguchi
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Diffusion Model, Data Augmentation, Autonomous Driving
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/ShogoNoguchi/AtteConDA)
- **Summary**: 本文针对多条件扩散模型中的条件冲突问题，提出了基于注意力的冲突抑制方法，用于自动驾驶任务的图像增强。该方法将语义分割、深度和边缘作为多条件输入，实现了更强的结构保持，为缓解高级自动驾驶任务的数据稀缺问题提供了重要进展。

<h3 id="495">[495] Evading Visual Aphasia: Contrastive Adaptive Semantic Token Pruning for Vision-Language Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.09429](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09429) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Jie Ma, Yihang Liu, Zhike Qiu, Jiayi Ji, Xiaoshuai Sun
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Vision-Language Model, Token Compression, LLM Inference
- **Summary**: 本文提出了COAST训练无关视觉标记剪枝框架，将压缩视为自适应语义路由，避免过早剪枝导致模型失去视觉接地而依赖语言先验的视觉失语症问题。该方法在七个基准上将视觉标记减少77.8%，实现2.15倍延迟加速，同时保持98.64%的原始平均性能。

<h3 id="496">[496] Key Coverage Matters: Semi-Structured Extraction of OCR Clinical Reports</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.09440](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09440) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yu Wang, Yingyun Li, Ying Qin, Haiyang Qian
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: OCR, Information Extraction, Medical AI
- **Summary**: 本文将临床报告提取问题形式化为基于OCR临床报告的规范键条件抽取式问答，引入键覆盖率作为衡量清单完整性的指标。使用0.2B BERT模型在20多家医院的真实报告上实验表明，性能随键覆盖率单调提升，Top-90规范键覆盖时F1分数达到0.839-0.893。

<h3 id="497">[497] SWIFT: Prompt-Adaptive Memory for Efficient Interactive Long Video Generation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.09442](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09442) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Shanwen Tan, Hao Li, Jingtao Zhang, Xiaosong Jia, Xue Yang, Shaofeng Zhang, Yanyong Zhang
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Video Generation, Diffusion Model
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/ShanwenTan/SWIFT)
- **Summary**: 本文提出SWIFT框架，一种无需训练的多提示长视频生成方法，通过语义注入缓存和自适应动态窗口实现高效的语义切换，同时保持时序连贯性。该方法在单张H100 GPU上达到22.6 FPS，显著提升了生成效率。

<h3 id="498">[498] RAwR: Role-Aware Rewiring via Approximate Equitable Partition</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.09457](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09457) (cross-listed)

- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.SI

<h3 id="499">[499] When Few Steps Are Enough: Training-Free Acceleration of Identity-Preserved Generation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.09460](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09460) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Dongqi Zheng
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Diffusion Model, Text-to-Image
- **Summary**: 本文发现身份保持的FLUX生成可以直接将冻结的InfuseNet适配器迁移到蒸馏后的backbone上，无需重新训练即可实现5.9倍加速，同时提升身份相似度。分析表明身份保真度在去噪早期即已建立。

<h3 id="500">[500] Align and Shine: Building High-Quality Sentence-Aligned Corpora for Multilingual Text Simplification</h3>

- **Authors**: Kenji Hilasaca, Nouran Khallaf, Serge Sharoff
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Text Simplification, Multilingual Learning
- **Venue**: LREC 2026 Workshop
- **Summary**: 本文报告了从可比语料库收集和处理众包简化数据的实验研究，构建了适用于多语言文本简化系统训练和测试的句子对齐语料库，涵盖加泰罗尼亚语、英语、法语、意大利语和西班牙语。

<h3 id="501">[501] Outlier-Robust Diffusion Solvers for Inverse Problems</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.09477](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09477) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yang Zheng, Jiahua Liu, Tongyao Pang, Wen Li, Zhaoqiang Liu
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Diffusion Model, Image Reconstruction
- **Venue**: CVPR 2026
- **Summary**: 本文提出了一种针对含异常值逆问题的鲁棒扩散模型求解方法，通过显式噪声估计和基于Huber损失的迭代加权最小二乘目标来处理异常值。实验表明该方法在多种图像数据集上表现出对异常值的鲁棒性。

<h3 id="502">[502] A Cognitively Grounded Bayesian Framework for Misinformation Susceptibility</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.09483](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09483) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Pranava Madhyastha
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Misinformation, Fake News Detection, Cognitive Science
- **Summary**: 本文提出了一个基于认知的贝叶斯框架——有界语用听者（BPL），用于建模人们对信息失序的易感性。该框架扩展了理性言语行为理论，引入了三个认知约束参数（递归深度限制、先验压缩参数和可用性样本量），并在LIAR和MultiFC基准数据集上验证了其在真实性分类任务上的有效性。

<h3 id="503">[503] CTQWformer: A CTQW-based Transformer for Graph Classification</h3>

- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Graph Neural Network, Quantum Computing
- **Summary**: 本文提出CTQWformer，一种将连续时间量子行走与GNN相结合的混合图学习框架，用于图分类任务。该方法使用可训练哈密顿量融合图拓扑和节点特征，在基准数据集上优于图核和GNN方法。

<h3 id="504">[504] APCD: Adaptive Path-Contrastive Decoding for Reliable Large Language Model Generation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.09492](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09492) (cross-listed)

<h3 id="505">[505] LASSA Architecture-Based Autonomous Fault-Tolerant Control of Unmanned Underwater Vehicles</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.09494](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09494) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Hong Chen, Zixiang Tang, Yuanbao Chen, Yu Liu
- **Subjects**: cs.RO; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Misinformation
- **Summary**: 本文提出了有界语用听众(BPL)框架，将理性言语行为理论与认知约束相结合，用于建模信息失序的易感性。该框架在LIAR和MultiFC基准上展示了具有竞争力的真实性分类性能。

<h3 id="506">[506] Spectral Transformer Neural Processes</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.09498](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09498) (cross-listed)

- **Summary**: 本文提出基于LASSA架构的智能控制方法，将LLM推理与求解器验证相结合，实现无人潜航器的自主容错控制。湖上实验表明该框架能成功检测故障并重新规划任务路径。

<h3 id="507">[507] Hidden Error Awareness in Chain-of-Thought Reasoning: The Signal Is Diagnostic, Not Causal</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.09502](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09502) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Aojie Yuan, Zhiyuan Julian Su, Haiyue Zhang, Yi Nian, Yue Zhao
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.LG

- **Summary**: 本文提出频谱Transformer神经过程(STNP)，通过频谱聚合器将频谱混合核偏置注入Transformer神经过程，有效建模周期性和准周期性。实验表明该方法在合成回归、真实时间序列和图像数据集上持续提升预测性能。

<h3 id="508">[508] Position: AI Security Policy Should Target Systems, Not Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.09504](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09504) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Michael A. Riegler, Inga Strümke
- **Subjects**: cs.CR; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: LLM Security, Jailbreak Attack, Multi-Agent System
- **Summary**: 本文提出swarm-attack框架，多个轻量级LLM代理通过共享内存和进化优化协同进行对抗测试。实验表明该方法能以零成本实现对前沿模型的安全绕过和软件漏洞发现。

<h3 id="509">[509] Mixture of Layers with Hybrid Attention</h3>

<h3 id="510">[510] Assessment of RAG and Fine-Tuning for Industrial Question-Answering-Applications</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.09533](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09533) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Jakob Sturm, Josef Pichlmeier, Christian Bernhard, Maka Karalashvili, Johannes Klepsch, Georg Groh, Andre Luckow

<h3 id="511">[511] Governing AI-Assisted Security Operations: A Design Science Framework for Operational Decision Support</h3>

<h3 id="512">[512] TAD: Temporal-Aware Trajectory Self-Distillation for Fast and Accurate Diffusion LLM</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.09536](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09536) (cross-listed)

- **Tags**: Diffusion Model, Knowledge Distillation, LLM Inference

- **Summary**: 本文提出TAD框架，一种时间感知的轨迹自蒸馏方法，通过将近端和远端token分区并分别应用硬交叉熵损失和软KL散度损失，改善扩散LLM的精度-并行性权衡。

<h3 id="514">[514] KAN Text to Vision? The Exploration of Kolmogorov-Arnold Networks for Multi-Scale Sequence-Based Pose Animation from Sign Language Notation</h3>

<h3 id="515">[515] Biosignal Fingerprinting: A Cross-Modal PPG-ECG Foundation Model</h3>

<h3 id="516">[516] CLR-voyance: Reinforcing Open-Ended Reasoning for Inpatient Clinical Decision Support with Outcome-Aware Rubrics</h3>

<h3 id="517">[517] Efficient Ensemble Selection from Binary and Pairwise Feedback</h3>

<h3 id="518">[518] SmartEval: A Benchmark for Evaluating LLM-Generated Smart Contracts from Natural Language Specifications</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.09610](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09610) (cross-listed)

- **Subjects**: cs.MA; cs.AI; cs.CE; cs.LG; cs.PL; cs.SE

<h3 id="519">[519] Any2Any 3D Diffusion Models with Knowledge Transfer: A Radiotherapy Planning Study</h3>

<h3 id="520">[520] Adaptive DNN Partitioning and Offloading in Heterogeneous Edge-Cloud Continuum</h3>

<h3 id="521">[521] RDEx-CASK: Cauchy Mutation, Archive, and Stagnation Kick for RDEx-CSOP</h3>

<h3 id="522">[522] MedMeta: A Benchmark for LLMs in Synthesizing Meta-Analysis Conclusion from Medical Studies</h3>

<h3 id="523">[523] Causal Parametric Drift Simulation: A Digital Twin Framework for Classifier Robustness Evaluation</h3>

<h3 id="524">[524] Learning Multi-Indicator Weights for Data Selection: A Joint Task-Model Adaptation Framework with Efficient Proxies</h3>

- **Summary**: 该论文提出了一种联合任务-模型自适应的数据选择框架，通过上下文学习信号在紧凑验证集上学习多指标权重，仅用30%训练样本即可达到全量数据调优的性能。

<h3 id="525">[525] Rethinking Evaluation of Multiple Sclerosis (MS) Lesion Segmentation Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.09666](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09666) (cross-listed)

<h3 id="526">[526] S2P-Net: A Spectral-Spatial Polar Network for Rotation-Invariant Object Recognition in Low-Data Regimes</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.09667](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09667) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Albert Heruth
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Object Recognition, Rotation Invariance

<h3 id="527">[527] ChaosNetBench: Benchmarking Spatio-Temporal Graph Neural Networks on Chaotic Lattice Dynamics</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.09676](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09676) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Henok Tenaw Moges, Charalampos Skokos, Deshendran Moodley
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; nlin.CD
