<h3 id="528">[528] DeepTumorVQA: A Hierarchical 3D CT Benchmark for Stage-Wise Evaluation of Medical VLMs and Tool-Augmented Agents</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.09679](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09679) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yixiong Chen, Wenjie Xiao, Pedro R. A. S. Bassi, Boyan Wang, Liang He, Xinze Zhou, Sezgin Er, Ibrahim Ethem Hamamci, Zongwei Zhou, Alan Yuille
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Vision-Language Model, Medical AI, Benchmark
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/Schuture/DeepTumorVQA)
- **Summary**: 该论文提出DeepTumorVQA分层基准，将3D CT推理分解为识别、测量、视觉推理和医学推理四个阶段，用于评估医学视觉语言模型和工具增强代理。

<h3 id="529">[529] MonitoringBench: Semi-Automated Red-Teaming for Agent Monitoring</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.09684](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09684) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Monika Jotautaitė, Maria Angelica Martinez, Ollie Matthews, Tyler Tracy
- **Subjects**: cs.CR; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Security, LLM Evaluation, Red Teaming
- **Summary**: 本文提出了一种半自动化红队测试方法，用于评估AI代理监控器的能力，解决了攻击生成中的模式崩溃、构思-执行差距和人工扩展成本高等问题。该方法生成的MonitoringBench基准包含2,644条攻击轨迹，揭示了前沿监控器常被说服性攻击欺骗或无法正确校准可疑性评分。

<h3 id="530">[530] Learning Unified Representations of Normalcy for Time Series Anomaly Detection</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.09685](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09685) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Prithul Sarker, Sushmita Sarker, Nicholas G. Murray, Alireza Tavakkoli
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Anomaly Detection, Time Series Analysis, Generative Model
- **Summary**: 本文提出了U²AD框架，通过基于分数的生成建模学习正常样本的潜在数据分布，用于多变量时间序列的无监督异常检测。该方法引入时间依赖的分数网络和统一训练目标，在检测准确性和早期异常识别方面均优于现有最先进方法。

<h3 id="531">[531] Do multimodal models imagine electric sheep?</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.09693](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09693) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Santhosh Kumar Ramakrishnan, Carl Vondrick, Raja Giryes, Philipp Krähenbühl, Vladlen Koltun
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Vision-Language Model, Visual Reasoning, World Model
- **Summary**: 研究发现大型多模态模型在解决空间谜题时会形成心理意象，模型在每个动作后的激活编码了关于中间状态的有意义视觉信息。作者提出将视觉token整合到思维链中，可将平均解决率从83%提升至89%。

<h3 id="532">[532] Adaptive Data Harvesting for Efficient Neural Network Learning with Universal Constraints</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.09707](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09707) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Siteng Kang, Xinhua Zhang
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Physics-Informed Learning, Reinforcement Learning, Data Selection
- **Summary**: 本文提出一种基于强化学习的方法，用于动态调整神经网络训练中的样本选择，以更好地满足通用约束（如Lyapunov神经网络和物理信息神经网络）。学习到的策略在提高约束满足度的同时显著提升了训练效率。

<h3 id="533">[533] Metal-Sci: A Scientific Compute Benchmark for Evolutionary LLM Kernel Search on Apple Silicon</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.09708](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09708) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Víctor Gallego
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.DC
- **Tags**: Code Generation, Benchmark, Scientific Computing
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/vicgalle/metal-sci-kernels)
- **Summary**: 本文提出了Metal-Sci，一个针对Apple Silicon Metal科学计算内核的10任务基准测试，涵盖6种优化场景（模板计算、n体问题、玻尔兹曼方程、分子动力学、PDE和FFT）。作者使用冻结的LLM驱动(1+1)进化循环自动搜索优化内核，并设计了一种保留门评分函数作为廉价监督机制，能够检测到在训练规模上表现良好但在未见配置上失效的静默回归问题。

<h3 id="534">[534] Distilling 3D Spatial Reasoning into a Lightweight Vision-Language Model with CoT</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.09719](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09719) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Alaa Asfour, Christopher Indris, Leihan Chen, Tejas Vyas, Guanghui Wang
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Knowledge Distillation, Vision-Language Model, 3D Vision
- **Summary**: 本文提出一种知识蒸馏框架，将3D空间推理能力从7B教师模型迁移到2.29B学生模型，实现8.7倍推理延迟降低和3倍模型尺寸缩减，同时保留54-72%的性能。框架引入了"Hidden CoT"潜在token作为内部推理草稿。

<h3 id="535">[535] Security Risks in Tool-Enabled AI Agents: A Systematic Analysis of Privileged Execution Environments</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.09721](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09721) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Hardik Goel
- **Subjects**: cs.CR; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Security, LLM Agent, Cybersecurity
- **Venue**: COMPSAC 2026 Workshop
- **Summary**: 本文系统分析了云托管AI代理在特权执行环境中的安全风险，引入风险类别分类法并通过三个代表性场景进行说明。分析表明风险主要源于过度特权的工具、能力-意图不匹配和执行环境中的环境权限泄露。

<h3 id="536">[536] One for All: A Non-Linear Transformer can Enable Cross-Domain Generalization for In-Context Reinforcement Learning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.09727](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09727) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Bowen He, Juncheng Dong, Lin Lin, Xiang Cheng
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: In-Context Learning, Reinforcement Learning, Transfer Learning
- **Summary**: 本文建立了非线性Transformer与基于核的时序差分学习之间的联系，证明同一RKHS中不同领域的价值函数可以用共享权重表示。MetaWorld域上的实验验证了时序差分目标的收敛性。

<h3 id="537">[537] Trajectory Supervision for Continual Tool-Use Learning in LLMs</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.09734](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09734) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Vishnu Vardhan Reddy, Sagnik Chatterjee, Soumik Bhatta
- **Subjects**: cs.SE; cs.AI; cs.MA
- **Tags**: Tool Learning, Continual Learning, Instruction Tuning
- **Summary**: 本文研究在LLM持续学习工具使用时，保留工具使用轨迹是否比仅保留最终结果更有帮助。实验表明，保留轨迹上下文可将最终精确调用准确率从39.2%提升至56.9%，但需要25.1%更多的训练token。

<h3 id="538">[538] KV-RM: Regularizing KV-Cache Movement for Static-Graph LLM Serving</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.09735](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09735) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Zhiqing Zhong, Zhijing Ye, Jian Zhang, Weijian Zheng, Bolun Sun, Xiaodong Yu
- **Subjects**: cs.AR; cs.AI; cs.DC; cs.OS
- **Tags**: LLM Serving, KV Cache, LLM Inference
- **Summary**: 本文提出KV-RM运行时设计，通过解耦逻辑KV历史与物理存储、使用块分页器跟踪活动KV状态，在静态图LLM解码器下规范化KV缓存移动。该方法在NVIDIA A100上改善了混合长度解码吞吐量和尾部延迟。

<h3 id="539">[539] The Silent Vote: Improving Zero-Shot LLM Reliability by Aggregating Semantic Neighborhoods</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.09739](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09739) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Sanket Badhe, Priyanka Tiwari, Deep Shah
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Inference, Zero-Shot Learning, Text Classification
- **Venue**: ACL 2026 Workshop
- **Summary**: 本文识别了零样本LLM分类中的"重归一化偏差"问题，提出Semantic Softmax方法通过聚合目标标签周围语义邻域的分数来恢复丢失的信息。该方法在GoEmotions和Civil Comments数据集上显著降低了期望校准误差和Brier分数。

<h3 id="540">[540] TIDES: Implicit Time-Awareness in Selective State Space Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.09742](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09742) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Taylan Soydan, Miguel A. Bessa, Dirk Mohr, Rui Barreira
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: State Space Model, Time Series Analysis, Time Series Classification
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/TaylanSoydan/TIDES)
- **Summary**: 本文提出TIDES，一种选择性状态空间模型变体，通过将输入依赖性转移到对角状态矩阵来协调选择性和连续架构。这使模型能够原生处理不规则时间戳，同时保持选择性SSM的有效性。

<h3 id="541">[541] Entropy-informed Decoding: Adaptive Information-Driven Branching</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.09745](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09745) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Benjamin Patrick Evans, Sumitra Ganesh, Leo Ardon
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.IT
- **Tags**: Remote Sensing, Image Segmentation, Feature Selection
- **Summary**: 本文提出一种基于序列前向浮动选择(SFFS)的特征选择框架，用于多光谱滑坡分割任务。该方法识别出一个紧凑的8通道子集，在使用更少通道的情况下匹配或超过30通道配置的分割性能。

<h3 id="542">[542] Sequential Feature Selection for Efficient Landslide Segmentation from Multi-Spectral Data</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.09746](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09746) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Arsalaan Ahmad, Oktay Karakus, Paul L. Rosin
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Inference, Decoding Strategy, Uncertainty Estimation
- **Venue**: ICML 2026
- **Summary**: 本文引入EDEN解码框架，根据模型不确定性自适应分配计算资源，在高熵区域扩展更多候选，在低熵区域采用更贪婪的路径。实验证明该方法在数学推理、代码生成等任务上实现了更好的准确性-扩展权衡。

<h3 id="543">[543] LEVI: Stronger Search Architectures Can Substitute for Larger LLMs in Evolutionary Search</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.09764](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09764) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Temoor Tanveer
- **Subjects**: cs.NE; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Evolutionary Computation, LLM Optimization, Program Optimization
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/ttanv/levi)
- **Summary**: 本文提出LEVI演化框架，通过更强的搜索架构替代更大的LLM，在解决方案多样性保持、智能变异路由和代理基准评估三方面进行改进。在系统研究基准上，LEVI以3.3-6.7倍更小的预算达到最高分数。

<h3 id="544">[544] WISTERIA: Learning Clinical Representations from Noisy Supervision via Multi-View Consistency in Electronic Health Records</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.09765](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09765) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Ruan Dong, Yuanyun Zhang, Shi Li
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Medical AI, Representation Learning, Weak Supervision
- **Summary**: 本文提出WISTERIA弱监督表示学习框架，将标签建模为潜在临床状态的随机观测，通过多视图一致性学习电子健康记录的临床表示。该方法在预测性能、标签噪声鲁棒性和跨机构泛化方面均优于序列预训练目标。

<h3 id="545">[545] Exploitation Without Deception: Dark Triad Feature Steering Reveals Separable Antisocial Circuits in Language Models</h3>

<h3 id="562">[562] Nautilus Compass: Black-box Persona Drift Detection for Production LLM Agents</h3>

<h3 id="563">[563] Continuous Latent Contexts Enable Efficient Online Learning in Transformers</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.09867](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09867) (cross-listed)

- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Visual Reasoning, Benchmark, Vision-Language Model
- **Summary**: 本文识别出视觉推理基准中的'笛卡尔捷径'漏洞，模型利用正交网格布局进行文本推理而非真正的视觉理解；引入Polaris-Bench在极坐标空间重新构建任务，揭示前沿模型准确率从70-83%崩溃至31-39%。

<h3 id="564">[564] Intervention-Based Time Series Causal Discovery via Simulator-Generated Interventional Distributions</h3>

<h3 id="565">[565] EgoMemReason: A Memory-Driven Reasoning Benchmark for Long-Horizon Egocentric Video Understanding</h3>

<h3 id="566">[566] Key-Value Means</h3>

<h3 id="567">[567] The Cartesian Shortcut: Re-evaluate Vision Reasoning in Polar Coordinate Space</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.09883](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09883) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Xia Hu, Zhenrui Yue, Brian Potetz, Howard Zhou, Leonidas Guibas, Chun-Ta Lu, Zhicheng Wang
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Vision-Language Model, Visual Reasoning, Benchmark
- **Summary**: 本文发现多模态大语言模型在视觉推理基准测试中存在"笛卡尔捷径"问题，即模型利用正交网格布局可被离散化为文本坐标的特性，依赖文本推理而非真正的视觉理解。作者提出Polaris-Bench基准，将视觉推理任务重构到极坐标空间以打破这一捷径。实验表明，在笛卡尔布局上达到70-83%准确率的模型在极坐标等效任务上骤降至31-39%，揭示了当前MLLM缺乏拓扑不变的视觉推理能力。

- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Feature Selection, GPU Computing
- **Summary**: 本文提出了两种GPU加速的Boruta特征选择算法版本：基于置换特征重要性的Boruta-Permut和基于不纯度减少的Boruta-TreeImp。实验表明这些方法在保持特征选择准确性的同时大幅提升了计算效率。

<h3 id="579">[579] SDTalk: Structured Facial Priors and Dual-Branch Motion Fields for Generalizable Gaussian Talking Head Synthesis</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.09956](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09956) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Peng Jia, Zhen Xiao, Jia Li, Xueliang Liu, Zhenzhen Hu, Lingyun Yu
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Video Generation, 3D Vision, Motion Synthesis
- **Summary**: 本文提出SDTalk，一种基于3D高斯泼溅的单次说话人头合成框架，可泛化到未见过的身份而无需个性化训练。该框架结合结构化面部先验和双分支运动场，在视觉质量和推理效率上超越现有方法。

<h3 id="580">[580] G-Zero: Self-Play for Open-Ended Generation from Zero Data</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.09959](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09959) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Chengsong Huang, Haolin Liu, Tong Zheng, Runpeng Dai, Langlin Huang, Jinyuan Li, Zongxia Li, Zhepei Wei, Yu Meng, Jiaxin Huang
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.CL; cs.ET
- **Tags**: Self-Improvement, Self-Play, LLM Reasoning
- **Summary**: 本文提出G-Zero，一个无需验证器的协同进化框架，用于LLM在开放性任务中的自主自我改进。通过Hint-δ内在奖励信号量化预测偏移，该框架绕过外部评判者的能力上限，实现跨不可验证领域的持续自我进化。

<h3 id="581">[581] HapticLDM: A Diffusion Model for Text-to-Vibrotactile Generation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.09971](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09971) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Jiahao Xiong, Fei Wang, Anran Xu, Pinzhi Huang, Tao Wen, Lijia Pan, Cai Chen
- **Subjects**: cs.HC; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Diffusion Model, Haptic Rendering
- **Summary**: 本文提出了HapticLDM，首个基于潜在扩散模型的文本到振动触觉生成模型，旨在解决从自然语言生成准确、一致且完整振动反馈的核心挑战。该方法引入了强调动态特性的文本处理策略和全局去噪机制，以实现连贯稳定的时域变化。实验评估表明，该模型在真实感和语义对齐方面优于现有自回归方法，并能简化触觉设计工作流程。

<h3 id="582">[582] GLiNER2-PII: A Multilingual Model for Personally Identifiable Information Extraction</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.09973](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09973) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Urchade Zaratiana, Ash Lewis, George Hurn-Maloney
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Information Extraction, Privacy, Data Synthesis
- **Summary**: 本文提出GLiNER2-PII，一个0.3B参数的多语言模型，用于识别42种PII实体类型。通过约束驱动的生成流程构建包含4910篇标注文本的多语言合成语料库，该模型在SPY基准上达到最高F1分数。

<h3 id="583">[583] Yeti: A compact protein structure tokenizer for reconstruction and multi-modal generation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.09981](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09981) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Nabin Giri, Steven Farrell, Kristofer E. Bouchard
- **Subjects**: q-bio.BM; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Protein Engineering, Multimodal Learning, Molecular Generation
- **Summary**: 本文提出Yeti，一种基于无查找量化的紧凑蛋白质结构分词器，采用流匹配目标进行端到端训练。该分词器在代码本利用率和令牌多样性方面表现优异，支持多模态模型联合生成蛋白质序列和结构。

<h3 id="584">[584] Geometric 4D Stitching for Grounded 4D Generation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.09984](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09984) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Sunwoo Park, Taesung Kwon, Jong Chul Ye
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: 3D Vision, Video Generation, 3D Reconstruction
- **Summary**: 本文提出几何4D拼接框架，显式识别缺失几何区域并用几何约束的4D拼接进行补充。该方法在单张RTX 5090 GPU上10分钟内完成4D场景表示构建，同时提高几何一致性并支持迭代扩展和编辑。

<h3 id="585">[585] Attention Drift: What Autoregressive Speculative Decoding Models Learn</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.09992](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09992) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Doğaç Eldenk, Payal Mohapatra, Yigitcan Comlek, Kaan Oktay, Hongyang Zhang, Stephen Xia
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Speculative Decoding, LLM Inference
- **Summary**: 本文识别出推测解码中的"注意力漂移"现象，即起草模型的注意力从提示逐渐转移到自身生成的令牌。作者提出两种架构改进（后归一化和每隐藏状态RMSNorm），显著提高了接受长度。

<h3 id="586">[586] Continual Harness: Online Adaptation for Self-Improving Foundation Agents</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.09998](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09998) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Seth Karten, Joel Zhang, Tersoo Upaa Jr, Ruirong Feng, Wenzhe Li, Chengshuai Shi, Chi Jin, Kiran Vodrahalli
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Self-Improvement, Game AI
- **Summary**: 本文提出Continual Harness，一个无重置的自我改进框架，使具身智能体能够在线适应并自动优化提示、子智能体、技能和记忆。在宝可梦游戏中，该系统从零开始显著降低了按键成本。

<h3 id="587">[587] Combining Mechanical and Agentic Specification Inference for Move</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.10005](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10005) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Wolfgang Grieskamp, Teng Zhang, Vineeth Kashyap
- **Subjects**: cs.PL; cs.AI; cs.LO; cs.SE
- **Tags**: Formal Methods, LLM Agent, Program Verification
- **Summary**: 本文描述了一个Move Prover的规范推断工具，结合了Move字节码的最弱前置条件分析和代理编码CLI。该工具通过MCP服务将WP分析暴露给编码代理，减少编写规范的样板代码。

<h3 id="588">[588] The two clocks and the innovation window: When and how generative models learn rules</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.10019](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10019) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Binxu Wang, Emma Lucia Byrnes Finn, Bingbin Liu
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.CC; stat.ML
- **Tags**: Deep Learning Theory, Generative Model
- **Venue**: NeurIPS 2025 Workshop
- **Summary**: 本文刻画了生成模型训练中的两个时间尺度：规则有效时间τ_rule和记忆时间τ_mem，定义了两者之间的"创新窗口"。研究表明该窗口随数据集增大而变宽，随规则复杂度增加而变窄。

<h3 id="589">[589] Medical Incident Causal Factors and Preventive Measures Generation Using Tag-based Example Selection in Few-shot Learning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.10025](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10025) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yuna Haseyama, Tomoki Ito, Hiroki Sakaji, Itsuki Noda
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Medical AI, Prompt Engineering, Few-Shot Learning
- **Summary**: 本文提出一种基于标签的少样本示例选择方法，用于提示LLM从医疗事故报告中生成背景/因果因素和预防措施。实验表明标签方法在精确度和稳定性方面优于随机采样和相似性选择。

<h3 id="590">[590] Speech-based Psychological Crisis Assessment using LLMs</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.10027](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10027) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Terumi Chiba, Yang Luo, Ziyun Cui, Yongsheng Tong, Chao Zhang
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Speech Processing, Mental Health, LLM Reasoning
- **Summary**: 本文提出一个基于LLM的心理危机等级自动分类框架，引入副语言注入方法将非言语情感线索插入语音转录中。结合推理增强训练策略，系统在三类分类任务上达到0.802的宏F1分数。

<h3 id="591">[591] Bridging the Cognitive Gap: A Unified Memory Paradigm for 6G Agentic AI-RAN</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.10036](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10036) (cross-listed)

- **Subjects**: cs.NI; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Memory Architecture, Wireless Networks, LLM Agent
- **Summary**: 本文提出统一内存范式，将生物记忆层次结构映射到异构计算架构上，用于6G代理AI-RAN。该方法用零拷贝可观测性替代消息传递，使AI代理能够弥合实时响应和长期上下文之间的差距。

<h3 id="592">[592] Personalizing LLMs with Binary Feedback: A Preference-Corrected Optimization Framework</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.10043](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10043) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Xilai Ma, Liye Zhao, Weijun Yao, Haibing Di, Wenya Wang, Jing Li
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Personalization, Preference Optimization, LLM Alignment
- **Venue**: ACL 2026
- **Summary**: 本文提出C-BPO框架，通过偏好校准的二值信号实现LLM个性化。该方法将目标用户数据视为正反馈，其他用户数据作为隐式负信号，并基于正未标记学习理论净化负信号，有效捕获用户间差异。

<h3 id="593">[593] Adaptive Action Chunking via Multi-Chunk Q Value Estimation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.10044](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10044) (cross-listed)

- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Reinforcement Learning, Action Chunking, Decision Making

<h3 id="594">[594] Rethinking Loss Reweighting for Imbalance Learning as an Inverse Problem: A Neural Collapse Point of View</h3>

- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Long-Tailed Learning, Neural Collapse, Optimization
- **Venue**: ICML 2026
- **Summary**: 本文从神经坍缩视角重新审视长尾分类中的损失重加权问题，将其建模为逆问题，提出了一种动态推断类别权重的方法以匹配理想等损失目标。

<h3 id="595">[595] Guided Streaming Stochastic Interpolant Policy</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.10051](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10051) (cross-listed)

- **Subjects**: cs.RO; cs.AI

- **Venue**: RSS 2026
- **Summary**: 本文提出了一种流式随机插值策略（SSIP），通过推导最优引导项实现实时机器人控制的推理时引导。该方法结合无训练和基于训练的引导机制，在动态环境中相比传统分块策略展现出更优的反应性和性能。

<h3 id="596">[596] Swarm Skills: A Portable, Self-Evolving Multi-Agent System Specification for Coordination Engineering</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.10052](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10052) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Xinyu Zhang, Zhicheng Dou, Deyang Li, Jianjun Tao, Shuo Cheng, Ruifeng Shi, Fangchao Liu, Enrui Hu, Yangkai Ding, Hongbo Wang, Qi Ye, Xuefeng Jin, Zhangchun Zhao
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Multi-Agent System, LLM Agent, Multi-Agent Coordination
- **Summary**: 本文提出了Swarm Skills规范，将多智能体协调协议转化为可移植、可分发的资产，并配套自演化算法自动提炼成功执行轨迹为新的协调技能。

<h3 id="597">[597] Not-So-Strange Love: Language Models and Generative Linguistic Theories are More Compatible than They Appear</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.10061](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10061) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: R. Thomas McCoy
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Cognitive Science, Linguistic Theory
- **Venue**: Behavioral and Brain Sciences
- **Summary**: 本文评论认为语言模型同样可以实例化基于形式结构的生成语言学理论，扩展了可用语言模型测试的理论空间，有望调和基于使用和生成式两种语言学观点。

<h3 id="598">[598] NCO: A Versatile Plug-in for Handling Negative Constraints in Decoding</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.10065](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10065) (cross-listed)

- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Inference, LLM Security, Text Generation
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/hyundong98/NCO-Decoding.git)
- **Summary**: 本文提出了NCO解码策略，通过在线模式匹配处理有限硬约束和正则约束，在不引起状态爆炸的情况下降低计算开销，有效抑制PII和亵渎内容生成。

<h3 id="599">[599] Metis: Learning to Jailbreak LLMs via Self-Evolving Metacognitive Policy Optimization</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.10067](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10067) (cross-listed)

- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Security, Jailbreak Attack, Red Teaming
- **Venue**: ICML 2026
- **Summary**: 本文提出了Metis框架，将越狱攻击重构为推理时策略优化问题，采用自演化元认知循环对目标防御逻辑进行因果诊断，在多个前沿模型上实现了最高的攻击成功率。

<h3 id="600">[600] PoDAR: Power-Disentangled Audio Representation for Generative Modeling</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.10084](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10084) (cross-listed)

- **Summary**: 本文提出了PoDAR框架，通过随机功率增强和潜在一致性目标将信号功率与不变语义内容解耦，使潜在空间更易建模，加速音频扩散模型收敛并提升生成质量。

<h3 id="601">[601] Retrieve-then-Steer: Online Success Memory for Test-Time Adaptation of Generative VLAs</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.10094](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10094) (cross-listed)

- **Summary**: 本文提出了一种在线成功记忆引导的测试时适应框架，使冻结的视觉-语言-动作模型能够通过检索和聚合历史成功执行片段来提升部署可靠性。

<h3 id="602">[602] HYPERPOSE: Hyperbolic Kinematic Phase-Space Attention for 3D Human Pose Estimation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.10100](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10100) (cross-listed)

- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Pose Estimation, 3D Vision, Attention Mechanism
- **Summary**: 本文提出了HYPERPOSE框架，在双曲空间中进行时空推理以原生保持人体骨骼的层次树拓扑结构，引入黎曼损失套件和不确定性加权课程来提升3D姿态估计精度。

<h3 id="603">[603] ViSRA: A Video-based Spatial Reasoning Agent for Multi-modal Large Language Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.10106](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10106) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Tingshu Mou, Jiabo He, Renying Wang, Ce Liu, Hao Yang, Tiehua Zhang, Jingjing Chen, Xingjun Ma
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Vision-Language Model, Spatial Reasoning, LLM Agent

<h3 id="604">[604] CFSPMNet: Cross-subject Fourier-guided Spatial-Patch Mamba Network for EEG Motor Imagery Decoding in Stroke Patients</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.10111](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10111) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Xiangkai Wang, Yun Zhao, Dongyi He, Qingling Xia, Gen Li, Xinlai Xing, Yuchi Pan, Bin Jiang
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.CV
- **Tags**: EEG Classification, Brain-Computer Interface, Domain Adaptation
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/wxk1224/CFSPMNet)
- **Summary**: 本文提出了CFSPMNet，一种用于中风患者运动想象脑电解码的跨患者适应框架。该方法结合傅里叶重组状态Mamba网络和共享-私有原型匹配，有效解决了病理神经重组导致的跨患者变异性问题，在两个中风运动想象脑电数据集上取得了显著的准确率提升。

<h3 id="605">[605] Think as Needed: Geometry-Driven Adaptive Perception for Autonomous Driving</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.10117](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10117) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Donghyun Kim, Jaehyoung Park
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Autonomous Driving, Object Detection, 3D Vision

<h3 id="606">[606] MicroWorld: Empowering Multimodal Large Language Models to Bridge the Microscopic Domain Gap with Multimodal Attribute Graph</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.10120](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10120) (cross-listed)

- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Vision-Language Model, Knowledge Graph, Scientific Reasoning
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/ieellee/MicroWorld)

<h3 id="607">[607] Explainability of Recurrent Neural Networks for Enhancing P300-based Brain-Computer Interfaces</h3>

- **Summary**: 本文提出了后循环模块(PRM)，通过全局和局部可解释性技术对P300脑电信号进行时空分析，在提升分类性能的同时揭示与神经生理学描述一致的时空特征。

<h3 id="608">[608] Scaling Vision Models Does Not Consistently Improve Localisation-Based Explanation Quality</h3>

- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI

<h3 id="609">[609] Coarsening Linear Non-Gaussian Causal Models with Cycles</h3>

<h3 id="610">[610] Task-Agnostic Noisy Label Detection via Standardized Loss Aggregation</h3>

<h3 id="611">[611] When Reviews Disagree: Fine-Grained Contradiction Analysis in Scientific Peer Reviews</h3>

- **Summary**: 本文解决了Kourovka笔记本中的开放问题，证明了每个有限群都存在一个"恰好有限"的表示——即删除任何关系都会导致无限群的表示。该研究得到了Google DeepMind AI数学工具的辅助。

<h3 id="644">[644] Can Language Models Analyze Data? Evaluating Large Language Models for Question Answering over Datasets</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.10419](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10419) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Andreas Xenofontos, Pavlos Fafalios
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Evaluation, Text-to-SQL, Question Answering
- **Venue**: CARMA 2026
- **Summary**: 本文研究了大型语言模型在数据集问答任务中的有效性，评估了直接回答问题和生成SQL查询两种场景。实验结果表明大型LLM表现强劲，而较小的资源高效模型存在明显局限性。

<h3 id="645">[645] Toward an Engineering of Science: Rebalancing Generation and Verification in the Age of AI</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.10425](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10425) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Jiaqi W. Ma
- **Subjects**: cs.CY; cs.AI
- **Tags**: AI Ethics, Scientific Publishing, AI Safety
- **Summary**: 本文讨论了AI生成科学制品带来的"认知污染"风险，提出将此视为工程问题来重新设计认知基础设施。作者提出了"蓝图"作为结构化的研究制品，通过前期生成成本换取更廉价的分布式验证。

<h3 id="646">[646] CoWorld-VLA: Thinking in a Multi-Expert World Model for Autonomous Driving</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.10426](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10426) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Minqing Huang, Yujiao Xiang, Zihan Liang, Jiajie Huang, Jingqi Wang, Zhi Xu, Feiyang Tan, Hangning Zhou, Mu Yang, Gong Che
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Autonomous Driving, World Model, Vision-Language Model
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/potatochip1211/CoWorld-VLA)
- **Summary**: 本文提出了CoWorld-VLA，一个用于自动驾驶的多专家世界推理框架，通过四种专家令牌（语义交互、几何结构、动态演化和自车轨迹）来引导动作规划。该方法采用基于扩散的分层多专家融合规划器，在NAVSIM v1基准上取得了竞争性结果。

<h3 id="647">[647] Physical probes expose and alleviate chemical-environment collapse in molecular representations</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.10429](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10429) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Jiebin Fang, Zidi Yan, Churu Mao, Yongjun Jiang, Xinyi Tang, Lei Miao, Dan Lu, Yun Huang, Wanjing Ding, Zhongjun Ma
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Molecular Generation, Representation Learning, Drug Discovery
- **Summary**: 本文揭示了分子表示学习中的"化学环境坍缩"问题，即分子拓扑等价的原子在真实化学环境中可能保持实验上的差异。作者提出了CLAIM框架，通过对比学习将拓扑分子输入与原子级NMR观测量对齐，有效缓解了这一问题。

<h3 id="648">[648] Real vs. Semi-Simulated: Rethinking Evaluation for Treatment Effect Estimation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.10430](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10430) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: George Panagopoulos
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; stat.ML
- **Tags**: Treatment Effect Estimation, Causal Inference, Model Evaluation
- **Summary**: 本文对处理效应估计评估进行了大规模实证研究，发现反事实指标无法可靠地恢复可观测指标偏好的估计器，且半模拟基准上的排名无法迁移到真实数据集。研究建议不应仅依赖反事实指标和半模拟基准来评估进展。

<h3 id="649">[649] StereoTales: A Multilingual Framework for Open-Ended Stereotype Discovery in LLMs</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.10442](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10442) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Pierre Le Jeune, Étienne Duchesne, Weixuan Xiao, Stefano Palminteri, Bazire Houssin, Benoît Malézieux, Matteo Dora
- **Subjects**: cs.CY; cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: Bias Mitigation, LLM Evaluation, Fairness
- **Summary**: 本文介绍了StereoTales，一个用于系统研究LLM开放式生成中社会偏见的多语言数据集和评估流程，覆盖10种语言和79个社会人口属性。研究发现所有评估模型都会在开放式生成中产生有害刻板印象，且提示语言强烈影响出现的刻板印象类型。

<h3 id="650">[650] Statistical Model Checking of the Keynes+Schumpeter Model: A Transient Sensitivity Analysis of a Macroeconomic ABM</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.10447](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10447) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Stefano Blando, Giorgio Fagiolo, Mauro Napoletano, Tania Treibich, Andrea Vandin
- **Subjects**: cs.MA; cs.AI; econ.GN; q-fin.ST
- **Tags**: Multi-Agent System, Economic Modeling, Formal Methods
- **Summary**: 本文展示了如何通过MultiVeStA实现统计模型检验（SMC），为宏观经济基于主体的模型（ABM）提供原则性的分析层。研究以启发式切换的凯恩斯+熊彼特（K+S）模型为案例，进行了瞬态敏感性分析，结果表明SMC能够支持对经济ABM进行可重复且有信息量的定量分析，同时使不确定性估计和模拟成本成为报告结果的显式部分。

<h3 id="651">[651] Cavity-Enhanced Collective Quantum Processing with Polarization-Encoded Qubits</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.10473](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10473) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Kamil Wereszczyński, Józef Cyran, Adam Brzezowski, Dawid Załużny, Robert Potoniec, Kasper Wiśniowski, Agnieszka Michalczuk
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Social Simulation, Multi-Agent System, Economic Modeling
- **Summary**: 本文将统计模型检验方法应用于宏观经济主体基模型（K+S模型）的分析，通过MultiVeStA实现了有原则的分析层。研究展示了不同参数族在瞬态效应上的明显差异，宏观金融和结构性参数扫描产生最强的瞬态效应。

<h3 id="652">[652] Formally Verifying Analog Neural Networks Under Process Variations Using Polynomial Zonotopes</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.10474](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10474) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yasmine Abu-Haeyeh, Tobias Ladner, Matthias Althoff, Lars Hedrich
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Quantum Computing
- **Summary**: 本文介绍了一种用于集体量子处理的腔增强光学架构，其中逻辑量子比特编码在循环腔内模式的偏振子空间中。分析表明，使用实验可及的固态非线性介质，在厘米级腔中可实现数量级为1的条件相位。

<h3 id="653">[653] DeepRefine: Agent-Compiled Knowledge Refinement via Reinforcement Learning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.10488](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10488) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Haoyu Huang, Jiaxin Bai, Shujie Liu, Yang Wei, Hong Ting Tsang, Yisen Gao, Zhongwei Xie, Yufei Li, Yangqiu Song
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Formal Methods, Neural Verification, Hardware Reliability
- **Summary**: 本文提出了一种基于多项式的模型来模拟工艺变化下的模拟神经网络行为，并使用多项式Zonotope进行可达性分析的形式化验证。该方法将验证时间从数天缩短到数秒，同时覆盖99%的变化样本。

<h3 id="654">[654] Multi-layer attentive probing improves transfer of audio representations for bioacoustics</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.10494](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10494) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Marius Miron, David Robinson, Masato Hagiwara, Titouan Parcollet, Jules Cauzinille, Gagan Narula, Milad Alizadeh, Ellen Gilsenan-McMahon, Sara Keen, Emmanuel Chemla, Benjamin Hoffman, Maddie Cusimano, Diane Kim, Felix Effenberger, Jane K. Lawton, Aza Raskin, Olivier Pietquin, Matthieu Geist
- **Subjects**: cs.SD; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Knowledge Management, Reinforcement Learning, Knowledge Editing
- **Summary**: 本文提出了DeepRefine，一个基于LLM的推理模型，用于智能体编译的知识库精炼。该模型通过多轮交互和溯因诊断定位缺陷并执行针对性精炼动作，使用强化学习和Gain-Beyond-Draft奖励进行端到端训练。

<h3 id="655">[655] Simultaneous Long-tailed Recognition and Multi-modal Fusion for Highly Imbalanced Multi-modal Data</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.10498](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10498) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Heegeon Yoon, Heeyoung Kim
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; stat.ML
- **Tags**: Bioacoustics, Representation Learning, Transfer Learning
- **Summary**: 本文系统研究了生物声学基准测试中的探测策略，评估了最后一层探测和多层探测、线性探测和注意力探测。结果表明，利用时间信息的较大探测头具有更优性能，多层探测在所有测试模型中都提升了下游任务性能。

<h3 id="656">[656] CMKL: Modality-Aware Continual Learning for Evolving Biomedical Knowledge Graphs</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.10510](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10510) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yousef A. Radwan, Yao Li, Qing Qing, Ziqi Xu, Qixin Zhang, Yongcheng Jing, Renqiang Luo, Xikun Zhang
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Knowledge Graph, Continual Learning, Mixture-of-Experts
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/yradwan147/cmkl-neurips2026)
- **Summary**: 本文提出了CMKL，一个用于生物医学知识图谱的持续学习框架，原生编码结构、文本和分子信息，通过混合专家路由器进行融合。该方法在持续生物医学实体分类上相比最强结构基线提升60%，且几乎零遗忘。

<h3 id="657">[657] SoK: A Systematic Bidirectional Literature Review of AI & DLT Convergence</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.10515](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10515) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Ali Irzam Kathia, Yimika Erinle, Abylay Satybaldy, Paolo Tasca, Nikhil Vadgama, Marco Alberto Javarone
- **Subjects**: cs.CR; cs.AI; cs.DC
- **Tags**: Survey, Blockchain
- **Summary**: 本文对2020-2025年间AI与分布式账本技术(DLT)融合的研究进行了双向系统性综述，将贡献分为AI增强的DLT和DLT增强的AI两个方向，并分别从五个层次进行分析。研究发现现有工作集中在少数层次，缺乏生产规模的部署验证，未来需要跨层协同设计和真实环境下的实证验证。

<h3 id="658">[658] Infinite Mask Diffusion for Few-Step Distillation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.10518](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10518) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Jaehoon Yoo, Wonjung Kim, Chanhyuk Lee, Seunghoon Hong
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Diffusion Model, Knowledge Distillation
- **Summary**: 本文提出无限掩码扩散模型(IMDM)，通过引入随机无限状态掩码来降低掩码扩散模型中的因子化误差下界，从而实现更好的少步蒸馏效果。实验表明IMDM在LM1B和OpenWebText上的少步生成任务中超越了现有方法。

<h3 id="659">[659] DuetFair: Coupling Inter- and Intra-Subgroup Robustness for Fair Medical Image Segmentation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.10521](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10521) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yiqi Tian, Sangjoon Park, Bo Zeng, Pengfei Jin, Yujin Oh, Quanzheng Li
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Medical Imaging, Fairness, Image Segmentation
- **Summary**: 这篇论文针对医学图像分割中的公平性问题，提出了DuetFair机制，这是一个双轴公平性框架，同时考虑组间适应性和组内鲁棒性。作者识别了"组内隐藏失败"问题，即子组内的高损失样本被子组平均值掩盖，并提出了FairDRO方法，结合分布感知的混合专家模型和子组条件分布鲁棒优化损失聚合，在多个医学图像分割基准上取得了优异的公平性性能。

<h3 id="660">[660] HH-SAE: Discovering and Steering Hierarchical Knowledge of Complex Manifolds</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.10536](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10536) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Honghan Wu, Tianyan Wang, Jiacong Mi, Zhoyang Jiang, Yunsoo Kim
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Interpretability, Representation Learning
- **Summary**: 本文提出混合层次稀疏自编码器(HH-SAE)，通过将流形分解为上下文层、原子层和组合层的嵌套层次结构来解决特征密度冲突问题。该方法在欺诈检测中实现了0.9156的跨域零样本AUC，并通过知识引导合成在AUPRC上提升了9.9%。

<h3 id="661">[661] ThreatCore: A Benchmark for Explicit and Implicit Threat Detection</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.10563](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10563) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Davide Bruni, Carlo Bardazzi, Maurizio Tesconi
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Benchmark, Content Moderation, Threat Detection
- **Summary**: 本文引入ThreatCore基准数据集，用于细粒度威胁检测，区分显性威胁、隐性威胁和非威胁。该数据集通过聚合多个公开资源并统一重新标注构建，实验表明隐性威胁比显性威胁更难检测，引入语义角色标注作为中间表示可以提升检测性能。

<h3 id="662">[662] Acceptance Cards:A Four-Diagnostic Standard for Safe Fine-Tuning Defense Claims</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.10575](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10575) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Phongsakon Mark Konrad, Toygar Tanyel, Serkan Ayvaz
- **Subjects**: cs.CR; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: LLM Security, Fine-Tuning, AI Safety
- **Summary**: 本文提出Acceptance Cards评估协议，用于验证安全微调防御声明，检查统计可靠性、新语义泛化、机制对齐和跨任务迁移四个诊断标准。在对SafeLoRA的审计中发现，在严格机制编码下它未能通过全部四个诊断。

<h3 id="663">[663] SenseBench: A Benchmark for Remote Sensing Low-Level Visual Perception and Description in Large Vision-Language Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.10576](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10576) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Chen Zhong, Xiao An, Jiaxing Sun, Zihan Gui, Guangyi Yang, Wei He
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Benchmark, Vision-Language Model, Remote Sensing
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/Zhong-Chenchen/SenseBench)
- **Summary**: 本文提出SenseBench，首个专门用于评估遥感低层视觉感知和描述的基准，包含超过10K个精心策划的实例，涵盖6大类22个细粒度遥感退化类别。对29个先进VLM的评估揭示了领域先验偏差、多失真崩溃、流畅性幻觉和感知-描述倒置效应等问题。

<h3 id="664">[664] Guaranteed Jailbreaking Defense via Disrupt-and-Rectify Smoothing</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.10582](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10582) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Zheng Lin, Zhenxing Niu, Haoxuan Ji, Haichang Gao
- **Subjects**: cs.CR; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Jailbreak Detection, LLM Security
- **Summary**: 本文提出Disrupt-and-Rectify Smoothing(DR-Smoothing)方法，通过两阶段提示处理方案(先破坏再纠正)来防御LLM的越狱攻击。该方法在保持有用性的同时提供可证明的防御成功概率边界，在白盒和黑盒设置下均能有效防御最先进的越狱攻击。

<h3 id="665">[665] An agentic framework for gravitational-wave counterpart association in the multi-messenger era</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.10584](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10584) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yiming Dong, Yacheng Kang, Junjie Zhao, Xinyuan Zhu, Ziming Wang, Lijing Shao
- **Subjects**: astro-ph.IM; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Scientific Discovery
- **Summary**: 本文开发GW-Eyes，一个由大语言模型驱动的智能体框架，首次集成领域特定工具并自主执行引力波与候选电磁事件之间的对应体关联任务。该框架支持自然语言交互，协助专家进行目录管理、天图可视化和快速验证等工作。

<h3 id="666">[666] CrackMeBench: Binary Reverse Engineering for Agents</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.10597](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10597) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Isaac David, Arthur Gervais
- **Subjects**: cs.SE; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Benchmark, Program Analysis, Reverse Engineering
- **Summary**: 本文引入CrackMeBench基准，用于评估语言模型智能体在教育性CrackMe风格逆向工程任务上的表现，专注于确定性二进制验证问题。在三模型评估中，GPT-5.5在生成任务分割上达到92%的pass@3，该基准提供了可复现的测试平台来衡量从源码推理到自主二进制分析的进展。

<h3 id="667">[667] Fairness vs Performance: Characterizing the Pareto Frontier of Algorithmic Decision Systems</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.10604](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10604) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Mieke Wilms, Christoph Heitz
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.CY
- **Tags**: Fairness, Decision Making
- **Venue**: FAccT 2026
- **Summary**: 本文将公平算法决策系统设计概念化为多目标优化问题，研究任意效用函数和群体公平性指标下的帕累托最优决策规则。研究发现帕累托前沿由应用于个体成功概率的确定性群体特定阈值规则组成，为评估和比较算法决策系统提供了理论基础。

<h3 id="668">[668] Measuring Embedding Sensitivity to Authorial Style in French: Comparing Literary Texts with Language Model Rewritings</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.10606](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10606) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Benjamin Icard, Lila Sainero, Alice Breton, Evangelia Zve, Jean-Gabriel Ganascia
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Authorship Attribution, Text Embedding
- **Venue**: NLP4DH 2026
- **Summary**: 本文使用受控的法国文学数据集研究嵌入对作者风格的敏感性，量化风格变化对嵌入分散度的影响。研究发现嵌入能够可靠地捕获作者风格特征，这些信号在重写后仍然保留，同时展现出LLM特有的模式，为语言模型时代的作者模仿检测提供了有前景的方向。

<h3 id="669">[669] Re-Triggering Safeguards within LLMs for Jailbreak Detection</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.10611](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10611) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Zheng Lin, Zhenxing Niu, Haoxuan Ji, Yuzhe Huang, Haichang Gao
- **Subjects**: cs.CR; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Jailbreak Detection, LLM Security
- **Summary**: 本文提出一种越狱提示检测方法，通过嵌入破坏来重新激活LLM内部的防御机制。与以往独立防御方法不同，该方法与LLM内部防御机制协作，通过重新触发来实现检测。实验表明该方法能有效防御白盒和黑盒设置下的最先进越狱攻击。

<h3 id="670">[670] Interpretable Coreference Resolution Evaluation Using Explicit Semantics</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.10627](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10627) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Bruno Gatti, Giuliano Martinelli, Roberto Navigli
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Information Extraction, Coreference Resolution
- **Venue**: ACL 2026
- **Summary**: 本文引入语义增强的指代消解评估框架，通过将概念和命名实体识别叠加到指代消解输出上，为名词性提及分配语义标签并传播到整个指代链。该框架能够计算按语义类别分层的分数，揭示聚合指标所掩盖的系统性弱点，并指导针对性的数据增强策略。

<h3 id="671">[671] Intrinsic Guardrails: How Semantic Geometry of Personality Interacts with Emergent Misalignment in LLMs</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.10633](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10633) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Krishak Aneja, Manas Mittal, Anmol Goel, Ponnurangam Kumaraguru, Vamshi Krishna Bonagiri
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Alignment, AI Safety, Interpretability
- **Summary**: 本文通过大五人格、黑暗三联征等心理测量特征映射LLM的潜在人格空间，发现隔离社会效价的方向(如'邪恶'人格向量和语义效价向量)可作为内在护栏：消融它们会导致错误对齐率超过40%，而放大它们可将失败模式抑制到3%以下。

<h3 id="672">[672] Towards Understanding Continual Factual Knowledge Acquisition of Language Models: From Theory to Algorithm</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.10640](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10640) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Haoyu Wang, Yifan Shang, Zhongxiang Sun, Weijie Yu, Xiao Zhang, Jun Xu
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Continual Learning, LLM Training
- **Venue**: ICML 2026
- **Summary**: 本文提出一个理论框架来刻画语言模型持续事实知识获取的训练动态，揭示正则化方法仅调整参数收敛速率而不改变遗忘倾向，而数据回放方法能成功转移收敛动态。基于此洞察，提出STOC方法通过注意力贡献选择有影响力的事实片段来指导回放数据生成。

<h3 id="673">[673] LLaVA-CKD: Bottom-Up Cascaded Knowledge Distillation for Vision-Language Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.10641](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10641) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Nikolaos Gkalelis, Vasileios Mezaris
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Knowledge Distillation, Vision-Language Model, Model Compression
- **Summary**: 本文提出了一种自底向上的级联知识蒸馏框架（CKD），用于解决视觉语言模型中师生网络容量差距过大的问题。该方法借鉴人类教育系统，引入中间容量的教师网络逐步引导学生学习，在多个VQA基准上取得了SOTA性能。

<h3 id="674">[674] A Recursive Decomposition Framework for Causal Structure Learning in the Presence of Latent Variables</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.10651](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10651) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Zheng Li, Feng Xie, Shenglan Nie, Xichen Guo, Ruxin Wang, Hao Zhang
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; stat.ML
- **Tags**: Causal Discovery, Causal Inference
- **Summary**: 本文提出了DiCoLa框架，将分治策略扩展到存在潜在变量的因果发现场景。该方法递归地将全局学习任务分解为更小的子问题，并通过原则性的重构步骤整合解决方案，在保证理论完备性的同时显著提升了计算效率。

<h3 id="675">[675] Active Learning for Gaussian Process Regression Under Self-Induced Boltzmann Weights</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.10654](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10654) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Jixiang Qing, Henry Moss, Matthias Sachs
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Active Learning, Gaussian Process, Drug Discovery
- **Summary**: 本文研究了在自诱导玻尔兹曼分布下的高斯过程回归主动学习问题，提出了AB-SID-iVAR获取函数，可在无需估计配分函数的情况下近似不可处理的贝叶斯目标分布。该方法在势能面建模和药物发现任务上展现了优越性能。

<h3 id="676">[676] When Can Digital Personas Reliably Approximate Human Survey Findings?</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.10659](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10659) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Mumin Jia, Yilin Chen, Divya Sharma, Jairo Diaz-Rodriguez
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.SI; stat.ML
- **Tags**: LLM Evaluation, User Simulation, Survey
- **Summary**: 本文研究了数字人格在多大程度上可以可靠地近似人类调查结果。通过LISS面板数据构建人格并测试其与人类回答的对齐程度，发现数字人格在低变异性问题上表现较好，但在个体预测和多变量结构恢复方面仍存在局限。

<h3 id="677">[677] bViT: Investigating Single-Block Recurrence in Vision Transformers for Image Recognition</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.10661](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10661) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Michal Byra, Pawel Olszowiec, Grzegorz Stefanski, Grzegorz Gruszczynski, Alberto Presta
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Vision Transformer, Parameter-Efficient Fine-Tuning, Image Classification
- **Summary**: 本文研究了单块循环视觉Transformer（bViT），其中一个Transformer块被重复应用于图像处理。实验表明，12步bViT-B在相同计算预算下可达到与标准ViT-B相当的准确率，同时参数量减少一个数量级。

<h3 id="678">[678] Prompt-Activation Duality: Improving Activation Steering via Attention-Level Interventions</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.10664](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10664) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Diancheng Kang, Zheyuan Liu, Ningshan Ma, Yue Huang, Zhaoxuan Tan, Meng Jiang
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Inference, KV Cache, Prompt Engineering
- **Summary**: 本文识别了多轮对话中激活引导的KV缓存污染问题，提出了门控裁剪注意力增量引导（GCAD）方法。该方法从系统提示的自注意力贡献中提取引导信号并应用token级门控，在多轮基准测试中显著提升了长期连贯性。

<h3 id="679">[679] Step Rejection Fine-Tuning: A Practical Distillation Recipe</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.10674](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10674) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Igor Slinko, Ilia Zavidnyi, Egor Bogomolov, Yaroslav Zharov
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.CL; cs.SE
- **Tags**: LLM Training, Instruction Tuning, Code Generation
- **Summary**: 本文提出了步骤拒绝微调（SRFT）方法，利用批评者LLM评估轨迹中每个步骤的正确性。在训练时对错误步骤屏蔽损失但保留上下文，使模型学会从错误中恢复而不重复错误，在SWE-bench上取得了32.2%的解决率。

<h3 id="680">[680] Is Data Shapley Not Better than Random in Data Selection? Ask NASH</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.10684](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10684) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Xiao Tian, Jue Fan, Rachael Hwee Ling Sim, Zixuan Wang, Nancy F. Chen, Bryan Kian Hsiang Low
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Data Selection, Data Valuation
- **Venue**: ICML 2026
- **Summary**: 本文提出了NASH数据选择框架，通过将目标效用函数分解为Shapley信息组件并非线性聚合来提升数据选择效果。该方法在最小额外运行时成本下显著提升了基于Shapley值的数据选择效果。

<h3 id="681">[681] The Bystander Effect in Multi-Agent Reasoning: Quantifying Cognitive Loafing in Collaborative Interactions</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.10698](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10698) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Dahlia Shehata, Ming Li
- **Subjects**: cs.MA; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Multi-Agent System, LLM Reasoning, LLM Evaluation
- **Summary**: 本文揭示了多智能体系统中的旁观者效应，发现模拟社会压力会引发严重的认知懈怠。研究形式化了交互深度极限和主权差距，证明模型经常在内部计算出正确推导但会主动服从社会压力。

<h3 id="682">[682] Why Low-Resource NLP Needs More Than Cross-Lingual Transfer: Lessons Learned from Luxembourgish</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.10714](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10714) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Fred Philippy, Siwen Guo, Jacques Klein, Tegawendé F. Bissyandé
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Low-Resource NLP, Cross-Lingual, Multilingual Learning
- **Venue**: ACL 2026 Workshop
- **Summary**: 本文综合了卢森堡语NLP研究，论证了跨语言迁移和语言特定努力应被视为互补而非竞争关系。跨语言迁移可显著提升目标语言性能，但其成功依赖于高质量的任务对齐目标语言数据。

<h3 id="683">[683] An Uncertainty-Aware Resilience Micro-Agent for Causal Observability in the Computing Continuum</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.10718](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10718) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Suvi De Silva, Alfreds Lapkovskis, Alaa Saleh, Sasu Tarkoma, Praveen Kumar Donta
- **Subjects**: cs.DC; cs.AI; cs.LG; cs.PF; eess.SY
- **Tags**: Edge Computing, Causal Inference, Fault Diagnosis
- **Summary**: 本文提出了AURORA框架，用于边缘计算环境中灰色故障的诊断和缓解。该框架整合自由能原理、因果do演算和局部因果状态图，支持反事实根因分析，并引入双门控执行机制确保高置信度时才执行修复。

<h3 id="684">[684] AllocMV: Optimal Resource Allocation for Music Video Generation via Structured Persistent State</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.10723](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10723) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Huimin Wang, Leilei Ouyang, Chang Xia, Yongqi Kang, Yu Fu, Yuqi Ouyang
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; cs.LG; cs.MA
- **Tags**: Video Generation, Resource Allocation, Multimodal Learning
- **Summary**: 本文提出了AllocMV框架，将音乐视频生成建模为多选择背包问题。通过结构化持久状态表示和基于显著性的资源分配，实现了在预算约束下感知质量与资源消耗的最优权衡。

<h3 id="685">[685] iPay: Integrated Payment Action Recognition via Multimodal Networks and Adaptive Spatial Prior Learning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.10732](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10732) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Kaicong Huang, Weiheng Oh, Thomas Guggisberg, Ruimin Ke
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Action Recognition, Multimodal Learning, Surveillance
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/ccoopq/iPay)
- **Summary**: 本文提出了iPay框架，用于公交支付行为识别。该框架采用多模态混合专家架构，结合RGB流、骨架流和双注意力融合流，并引入空间差异判别器显式建模手到锚点的相对运动，在真实监控数据上达到83.45%的识别准确率。

<h3 id="686">[686] Geospatial-Temporal Sensemaking of Remote Sensing Activity Detections with Multimodal Large Language Model</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.10739](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10739) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: David F. Ramirez, Tim Overman, Kristen Jaskie, Andreas Spanias
- **Subjects**: eess.IV; cs.AI; cs.CV
- **Tags**: Remote Sensing, Vision-Language Model, Question Answering
- **Venue**: SPIE 2026
- **Summary**: 本文介绍了SMART-HC-VQA数据集，用于遥感人类活动的时空分析。该数据集将建筑工地标注转换为自然语言问答三元组，包含约230万个双图像时序比较示例，并实现了多图像MLLM训练框架。

<h3 id="687">[687] Provable Sparse Inversion and Token Relabel Enhanced One-shot Federated Learning with ViTs</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.10748](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10748) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Li Shen, Xiaolei Hao, Qinglun Li, Xiaochun Cao, Zhifeng Hao, Xun Yang
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Federated Learning, Vision Transformer, Data Synthesis
- **Summary**: 本文提出了FedMITR框架，用于Vision Transformer的单次联邦学习。该方法采用稀疏模型反演生成数据，并通过token重标记策略解决非IID设置下的语义错位问题，理论分析证明了更紧的泛化边界。

<h3 id="688">[688] GridProbe: Posterior-Probing for Adaptive Test-Time Compute in Long-Video VLMs</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.10762](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10762) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Mohamed Eltahir, Lama Ayash, Ali Habibullah, Tanveer Hussain, Naeemullah Khan
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Video Understanding, Vision-Language Model, LLM Inference
- **Summary**: 本文提出了GridProbe方法，用于长视频视觉语言模型的自适应测试时计算。该方法通过后验探测在答案空间评分证据，自适应选择问题相关帧，在Video-MME-v2上以3.36倍TFLOPs减少达到与基线相当的准确率。

<h3 id="689">[689] Break the Brake, Not the Wheel: Untargeted Jailbreak via Entropy Maximization</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.10764](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10764) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Mengqi He, Xinyu Tian, Xin Shen, Shu Zou, Jinhong Ni, Zhaoyuan Yang, Weikang Li, Xuesong Li, Jing Zhang
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Jailbreak Attack, LLM Security, Vision-Language Model
- **Summary**: 本文提出了一种针对视觉语言模型的无目标越狱攻击方法UJEM-KL，通过最大化决策token的熵来翻转拒绝结果。该方法在三个VLM和两个安全基准上实现了有竞争力的白盒攻击成功率，并持续提高了跨模型迁移性。

<h3 id="690">[690] Dynamic Cross-Modal Prompt Generation for Multimodal Continual Instruction Tuning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.10765](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10765) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Tao Hu, Da-Wei Zhou
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Continual Learning, Vision-Language Model, Instruction Tuning
- **Summary**: 本文提出了DRAPE框架，用于多模态持续指令学习，通过动态生成实例特定的跨模态软提示来适应单个查询-图像对。该方法使用零空间梯度投影和基于CLIP的原型路由来缓解灾难性遗忘，在MCIT基准上取得了最先进的性能。

<h3 id="691">[691] MPerS: Dynamic MLLM MixExperts Perception-Guided Remote Sensing Scene Segmentation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.10769](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10769) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Ziyi Wang, Xianping Ma, Ziyao Wang, Hongyang Zhang, Man On Pun
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Remote Sensing, Image Segmentation, Vision-Language Model, Mixture-of-Experts
- **Venue**: CVPR 2026 Findings
- **Summary**: 本文提出了动态MLLM混合专家感知引导的遥感场景分割方法MPerS，通过设计多种提示让MLLM从不同专家视角感知遥感场景，并使用动态混合专家模块自适应地整合最有效的文本语义信息。该方法在三个公开语义分割遥感数据集上取得了优异性能。

<h3 id="692">[692] Towards a Large Language-Vision Question Answering Model for MSTAR Automatic Target Recognition</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.10772](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10772) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: David F. Ramirez, Tim L. Overman, Kristen Jaskie, Marv Kleine, Andreas Spanias
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; eess.IV
- **Tags**: Remote Sensing, Vision-Language Model, Question Answering
- **Venue**: SPIE DCS 2026
- **Summary**: 本文研究了大语言视觉模型在合成孔径雷达(SAR)图像自动目标识别中的应用，开发了基于MSTAR数据集的SAR训练和评估基准，包含描述性文本标题和问答对。通过参数高效微调，该方法在识别细粒度目标属性方面达到了98%的准确率。

<h3 id="693">[693] Beyond the Last Layer: Multi-Layer Representation Fusion for Visual Tokenizatio</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.10780](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10780) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Xuanyu Zhu, Yan Bai, Yang Shi, Yihang Lou, Yuanxing Zhang, Jing Jin, Yuan Zhou
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Vision Transformer, Representation Learning, Text-to-Image
- **Summary**: 本文提出了DRoRAE，一种深度路由表示自编码器，通过能量约束路由和增量校正自适应地聚合所有编码器层，以恢复在最后一层丢失的低级视觉细节。该方法在ImageNet-256上将rFID从0.57降至0.29，并发现融合容量与重建质量之间存在对数线性缩放定律。

<h3 id="694">[694] Can You Keep a Secret? Involuntary Information Leakage in Language Model Writing</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.10794](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10794) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Ari Holtzman, Peter West
- **Subjects**: cs.CR; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Security, Privacy
- **Summary**: 该论文研究了语言模型在被指示保密时是否能真正保守秘密。作者通过让模型在写故事时隐藏一个秘密词来测试，发现所有五个前沿模型都会通过主题选择、意象和场景等方式非自愿地泄露秘密信息，泄露率高达79%，即使秘密词从未字面出现。研究表明，当模型关注某个秘密时，会打开一个无法关闭的信息通道，即使被指示隐藏也无法阻止泄露。

<h3 id="695">[695] The Last Word Often Wins: A Format Confound in Chain-of-Thought Corruption Studies</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.10799](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10799) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Gabriel Garcia
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: LLM Reasoning, Chain-of-Thought, LLM Evaluation
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/Gpgabriel25/LastWordWinsCoT)
- **Summary**: 本文识别了思维链腐败研究中的一个系统性混淆因素：对于带有明确终端答案陈述的链，腐败研究检测的是答案文本出现的位置而非计算发生的位置。作者提出了一个三前提协议作为基于腐败的忠实性研究的最低标准。

<h3 id="696">[696] PhyGround: Benchmarking Physical Reasoning in Generative World Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.10806](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10806) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Juyi Lin, Arash Akbari, Yumei He, Lin Zhao, Haichao Zhang, Arman Akbari, Xingchen Xu, Zoe Y. Lu, Enfu Nan, Hokin Deng, Edmund Yeh, Sarah Ostadabbas, Yun Fu, Jennifer Dy, Pu Zhao, Yanzhi Wang
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Video Generation, Benchmark, Physical Reasoning
- **Summary**: 本文介绍了PhyGround，一个用于评估视频生成中物理推理的标准基准，包含250个精选提示和13条物理定律的分类法。作者还发布了PhyJudge-9B，一个开放的专业物理VLM评判器，其聚合相对偏差显著低于Gemini-3.1-Pro。

<h3 id="697">[697] Threat Modelling using Domain-Adapted Language Models: Empirical Evaluation and Insights</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.10808](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10808) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Saba Pourhanifeh, AbdulAziz AbdulGhaffar, Ashraf Matrawy
- **Subjects**: cs.CR; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Threat Modeling, Cybersecurity, LLM Evaluation
- **Summary**: 本文系统评估了领域适应语言模型在STRIDE威胁建模中的表现，分析了领域适应、模型规模、解码策略和提示技术对威胁分类的影响。结果表明领域适应模型并不持续优于通用模型，当前LLM在结构化威胁建模任务中存在根本性限制。

<h3 id="698">[698] Switching-Geometry Analysis of Deflated Q-Value Iteration</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.10811](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10811) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Donghwan Lee
- **Subjects**: math.OC; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Reinforcement Learning, Optimization
- **Summary**: 本文开发了联合谱半径框架来分析折扣马尔可夫决策过程控制中的降维Q值迭代，给出了首个基于JSR的降维Q-VI收敛性分析。分析表明降维的好处不是改变决策问题，而是提供了移除冗余全一分量后更精确的收敛几何描述。

<h3 id="699">[699] Policy Gradient Methods for Non-Markovian Reinforcement Learning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.10816](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10816) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Avik Kar, Siddharth Chandak, Rahul Singh, Soumitra Sinhahajari, Eric Moulines, Shalabh Bhatnagar, Nicholas Bambos
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Reinforcement Learning, Policy Gradient
- **Summary**: 本文研究了非马尔可夫决策过程中的策略梯度方法，提出了以奖励为中心的公式，联合优化智能体状态动态和控制策略。作者建立了ASM策略的新策略梯度定理，并提出了ASMPG算法，在非马尔可夫任务上优于基线方法。

<h3 id="700">[700] ALAM: Algebraically Consistent Latent Transitions for Vision-Language-Action Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.10819](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10819) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Zuojin Tang, Haoyun Liu, Xinyuan Chang, Changjie Wu, Dongjie Huo, Yandan Yang, Bin Liu, Zhejia Cai, Feng Xiong, Mu Xu, jiachen Luo, De Ma, Zhiheng Ma, Gang Pan
- **Subjects**: cs.RO; cs.AI; cs.CV
- **Tags**: Robotics, Vision-Language Model, World Model
- **Summary**: 本文提出了ALAM，一种代数一致的潜在动作模型，通过组合和反转一致性正则化从无动作视频中学习结构化潜在转移。该方法将机器人操作成功率从47.9%提高到85.0%，并展示了代数结构化潜在转移与联合流匹配之间的协同作用。

<h3 id="701">[701] SLIM: Sparse Latent Steering for Interpretable and Property-Directed LLM-Based Molecular Editing</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.10831](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10831) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Mingxu Zhang, Yuhan Li, Lujundong Li, Dazhong Shen, Hui Xiong, Ying Sun
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.CE; cs.CL
- **Tags**: Molecular Generation, LLM, Interpretability
- **Summary**: 本文提出了SLIM，一个即插即用的分子编辑框架，通过带有可学习重要性门的稀疏自编码器将编辑器的隐藏状态分解为稀疏的属性对齐特征。在该稀疏特征空间中引导可以精确激活属性相关维度，在MolEditRL基准上提高了高达42.4个点。

<h3 id="702">[702] MMVIAD: Multi-view Multi-task Video Understanding for Industrial Anomaly Detection</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.10833](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10833) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Xiran Zhao, Jing Jin, Yan Bai, Zhongan Wang, Yifeng Sun, Yihang Lou, Xuanyu Zhu, Tao Feng, Yingna Wu
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Anomaly Detection, Video Understanding, Industrial AI
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/Georgekeepmoving/MMVIAD)
- **Summary**: 本文介绍了MMVIAD，首个用于工业异常检测和理解的多视角多任务视频数据集，包含48个物体类别、14个环境和6种结构异常类型。作者开发了VISTA模型，在MMVIAD-Unseen上将基础模型的平均得分从45.0提高到57.5，超越GPT-5.4。

<h3 id="703">[703] Clin-JEPA: A Multi-Phase Co-Training Framework for Joint-Embedding Predictive Pretraining on EHR Patient Trajectories</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.10840](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10840) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yixuan Yang, Mehak Arora, Ryan Zhang, Baraa Abed, Junseob Kim, Tilendra Choudhary, Md Hassanuzzaman, Kevin Zhu, Ayman Ali, Chengkun Yang, Alasdair Edward Gent, Victor Moas, Rishikesan Kamaleswaran
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; q-bio.QM
- **Tags**: Medical AI, Representation Learning, Pre-training
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/YeungYathin/Clin-JEPA)
- **Summary**: 本文提出了Clin-JEPA，一个用于EHR患者轨迹联合嵌入预测预训练的多阶段协同训练框架。该方法在MIMIC-IV ICU数据上实现了稳定的潜在轨迹预测，在多任务下游评估中平均AUROC达到0.851和0.883。

<h3 id="704">[704] Training-Free Cultural Alignment of Large Language Models via Persona Disagreement</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.10843](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10843) (cross-listed)

- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.CY
- **Tags**: LLM Alignment, Cultural Alignment
- **Summary**: 本文介绍了DISCA，一种推理时文化对齐方法，通过实例化基于世界价值观调查的人格代理并将其分歧转换为有界的损失厌恶logit校正。该方法在20个国家和7个开源模型上减少了10-24%的文化错位，无需微调。

<h3 id="705">[705] Rethinking Agentic Search with Pi-Serini: Is Lexical Retrieval Sufficient?</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.10848](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10848) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Tz-Huan Hsu, Jheng-Hong Yang, Jimmy Lin
- **Subjects**: cs.IR; cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Information Retrieval, RAG
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/justram/pi-serini)
- **Summary**: 本文介绍了Pi-Serini，一个配备检索、浏览和阅读工具的搜索代理，研究表明当与能力更强的LLM（如GPT-5.5）配合使用时，经过良好配置的词汇检索器（BM25）可以实现83.1%的答案准确率和94.7%的证据召回率，优于使用稠密检索器的搜索代理。

<h3 id="706">[706] BEACON: A Multimodal Dataset for Learning Behavioral Fingerprints from Gameplay Data</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.10867](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10867) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Ishpuneet Singh, Gursmeep Kaur, Uday Pratap Singh Atwal, Guramrit Singh, Gurjot Singh, Maninder Singh
- **Subjects**: cs.CR; cs.AI; cs.CV; cs.LG; cs.NI
- **Tags**: Biometric Security, Multimodal Learning, Dataset
- **Summary**: 本文介绍了BEACON，一个大规模多模态数据集，用于在竞技游戏Valorant中学习行为指纹以实现持续身份认证。该数据集包含约430GB的同步模态数据，涵盖79个会话和28名玩家，包括鼠标动态、击键事件、网络数据包捕获、屏幕录制等多种数据类型。

<h3 id="707">[707] Attractor-Vascular Coupling Theory: Formal Grounding and Empirical Validation for AAMI-Standard Cuffless Blood Pressure Estimation from Smartphone Photoplethysmography</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.10871](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10871) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Timothy Oladunni, Farouk Ganiyu Adewumi
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Medical AI, Explainable AI, Biosignal Processing
- **Summary**: 本文提出了吸引子-血管耦合理论（AVCT），一个用于无袖带血压估计的数学框架，并通过智能手机光电容积脉搏波（PPG）进行验证。该模型在AAMI标准下实现了收缩压MAE 2.05 mmHg和舒张压MAE 1.67 mmHg，证明了仅使用智能手机摄像头即可实现临床级血压追踪。

<h3 id="708">[708] CADBench: A Multimodal Benchmark for AI-Assisted CAD Program Generation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.10873](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10873) (cross-listed)

- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: CAD Generation, Benchmark, Multimodal Learning
- **Summary**: 本文介绍了CADBench，一个用于多模态CAD程序生成的统一基准测试，包含18,000个评估样本，涵盖六个基准系列、五种输入模态和六种评估指标。研究发现专用网格到CAD模型在理想输入下显著优于代码生成VLM，并揭示了三种常见失败模式。

<h3 id="709">[709] AssayBench: An Assay-Level Virtual Cell Benchmark for LLMs and Agents</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.10876](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10876) (cross-listed)

- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; q-bio.QM
- **Tags**: Benchmark, Computational Biology, Drug Discovery
- **Summary**: 本文介绍了AssayBench，一个用于表型筛选预测的基准测试，旨在推动虚拟细胞模型的发展。该基准基于1,920个公开的CRISPR筛选数据，涵盖五大类细胞表型，将筛选预测任务形式化为基因排名预测问题。实验结果表明，现有方法距离性能上限仍有较大差距，零样本通用大语言模型的表现优于生物学专用模型。

<h3 id="710">[710] LoKA: Low-precision Kernel Applications for Recommendation Models At Scale</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.10886](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10886) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Liang Luo, Yinbin Ma, Quanyu Zhu, Vasiliy Kuznetsov, Yuxin Chen, Jian Jiao, Jiecao Yu, Buyun Zhang, Tongyi Tang, Xiaohan Wei, Yanli Zhao, Zeliang Chen, Yuchen Hao, Venkatesh Ranganathan, Sandeep Parab, Yantao Yao, Maxim Naumov, Chunzhi Yang, Shen Li, Ellie Wen, Wenlin Chen, Santanu Kolay, Chunqiang Tang
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI

<h3 id="711">[711] Shields to Guarantee Probabilistic Safety in MDPs</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.10888](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10888) (cross-listed)

<h3 id="712">[712] Unmasking On-Policy Distillation: Where It Helps, Where It Hurts, and Why</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.10889](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10889) (cross-listed)

<h3 id="713">[713] DataMaster: Towards Autonomous Data Engineering for Machine Learning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.10906](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10906) (cross-listed)

<h3 id="714">[714] Engineering Robustness into Personal Agents with the AI Workflow Store</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.10907](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10907) (cross-listed)

<h3 id="715">[715] Confidence-Guided Diffusion Augmentation for Enhanced Bangla Compound Character Recognition</h3>

<h3 id="716">[716] Variational Inference for Lévy Process-Driven SDEs via Neural Tilting</h3>

<h3 id="717">[717] ELF: Embedded Language Flows</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.10938](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10938) (cross-listed)

- **Tags**: Diffusion Model, Text Generation, Flow Matching
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/lillian039/ELF)
- **Summary**: 本文提出了嵌入式语言流（ELF），一类基于连续时间流匹配的连续嵌入空间扩散语言模型。与现有离散扩散语言模型不同，ELF主要在连续嵌入空间中操作直到最后一步，使得分类器无指导等技术可以直接应用，在生成质量和采样效率上优于现有方法。

- **Summary**: 本文提出使用情境判断测试和多维项目反应理论来测量LLM在角色条件下的稳定行为倾向。研究发现角色条件行为在多次运行中稳定，潜在特质分数能预测外部基准测试结果，为LLM行为评估提供了可靠替代方案。

<h3 id="730">[730] Consensus Sampling for Safer Generative AI</h3>

- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: AI Safety, Generative Model
- **Summary**: 本文提出共识采样算法，一种用于聚合多个概率分布以提升安全性的黑盒方法。该方法在存在不安全成分时仍能保持与最安全分布相当的竞争性能，为生成模型安全提供了模型无关的解决方案。

<h3 id="731">[731] Counting Still Counts: Understanding Neural Complex Query Answering Through Query Relaxation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2511.22565](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.22565) (replaced)

- **Summary**: 本文通过比较神经CQA模型与查询松弛策略，批判性地检验了神经方法学习泛化模式的假设。研究发现神经模型未能一致地超越松弛方法，且两者输出重叠很少，结合使用可提升性能。

<h3 id="732">[732] A Benchmark for Evaluating Outcome-Driven Constraint Violations in Autonomous AI Agents</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2512.20798](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.20798) (replaced)

<h3 id="733">[733] Recursive Language Models</h3>

<h3 id="734">[734] Batch-of-Thought: Cross-Instance Learning for Enhanced LLM Reasoning</h3>

<h3 id="735">[735] The Homogenization Problem in LLMs: Towards Meaningful Diversity in AI Safety</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2601.06116](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.06116) (replaced)

- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.CL; cs.CY

- **Summary**: 本文论证同质化应成为AI安全的核心关注点，并引入一个框架让利益相关者编码其情境和价值体系。作者从酷儿理论出发形式化同质化概念，并提出异种繁殖作为促进多样性的任务类别。

<h3 id="736">[736] Expert Evaluation and the Limits of Human Feedback in Mental Health AI Safety Testing</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2601.18061](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.18061) (replaced)

<h3 id="737">[737] Concise Geometric Description as a Bridge: Unleashing the Potential of LLM for Plane Geometry Problem Solving</h3>

<h3 id="738">[738] Emergence of Physical Intelligence via Controllable Information Production</h3>

<h3 id="739">[739] SayNext-Bench: Why Do LLMs Struggle with Next-Utterance Anticipation?</h3>

<h3 id="743">[743] When Agents Say One Thing and Do Another: Validating Elicited Beliefs from LLMs</h3>

<h3 id="817">[817] Where Do Reasoning Models Refuse?</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2507.03167](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.03167) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Kureha Yamaguchi, Benjamin Etheridge, Andy Arditi
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: LLM Reasoning, LLM Security, Interpretability
- **Venue**: ICML 2025 Workshop
- **Summary**: 本文研究推理模型在思维链过程中何时决定拒绝有害请求，发现蒸馏模型中思维链开头的细微差异可完全决定拒绝决策，并提取出线性拒绝方向。研究表明思维链对拒绝结果具有因果影响，但消融这些方向在推理模型上的效果不如非推理模型可靠。

<h3 id="818">[818] Causal Discovery for Irregularly Time Series with Consistency Guarantees</h3>

- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Causal Discovery, Time Series Analysis, Causal Inference
- **Summary**: 本文提出ReTimeCausal框架，用于不规则采样时间序列的因果发现，通过EM方法交替进行数据填补和结构学习，确保两者的一致性。该方法提供了结构恢复的理论一致性保证，在具有挑战性的不规则采样和缺失数据条件下优于现有方法。

<h3 id="819">[819] Elite Polarization in European Parliamentary Speeches: a Novel Measurement Approach Using Large Language Models</h3>

- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI

<h3 id="820">[820] Mitigating Watermark Forgery in Generative Models via Randomized Key Selection</h3>

- **Summary**: 本文主张停止使用人类心理和教育测试来评估AI系统，认为这种做法构成本体论错误。作者呼吁开发专门针对AI系统的原则性评估框架，这些框架可基于心理测量学测试的构建和验证框架，或完全从头创建以适应AI的独特语境。

- **Summary**: 本文提出了TetraJet-v2，一种端到端的4位全量化LLM训练方法。通过无偏双块量化、OsciReset振荡抑制和OutControl异常值控制算法，将BF16的性能差距缩小51.3%，同时实现1.67倍加速。

<h3 id="860">[860] Forget BIT, It is All about TOKEN: Towards Semantic Information Theory for LLMs</h3>

<h3 id="861">[861] Continuum: Efficient and Robust Multi-Turn LLM Agent Scheduling with KV Cache Time-to-Live</h3>

- **Summary**: 本文提出了Continuum系统，通过KV缓存生存时间机制优化多轮智能体工作负载的作业完成时间。该方法选择性保留KV缓存，在真实世界智能体任务上实现了超过8倍的平均作业完成时间改进。

<h3 id="863">[863] MURPHY: Feedback-Aware GRPO with Retrospective Credit Assignment for Multi-Turn Code Generation</h3>

<h3 id="864">[864] Energy Consumption of Dataframe Libraries for End-to-End Deep Learning Pipelines:A Comparative Analysis</h3>

<h3 id="872">[872] A probabilistic framework for crystal structure denoising, phase classification, and order parameters</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2512.11077](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.11077) (replaced)

- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Physics Simulation, Crystal Structure Analysis
- **Summary**: 该论文提出了一个统一的概率框架，用于分析原子模拟中含噪的晶体结构数据，能够同时实现去噪、相分类和序参数构建。模型通过预测每个原子和原型的对数几率，生成标量对数概率景观，其梯度定义保守去噪场，同时提供局部相标签和序参数。该方法在多种复杂场景下验证了其有效性，包括有限温度无序、点缺陷、水-冰共存和冲击压缩钛等。

- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI

- **Summary**: 本文提出一个统一的概率框架，用于分析噪声原子构型的晶体结构去噪、相分类和序参量提取。该模型通过每原子、每原型的对数概率预测，提供保守去噪场和局部相标签，能够跟踪晶体相变路径。

<h3 id="877">[877] LENS: LLM-Enabled Narrative Synthesis for Mental Health by Aligning Multimodal Sensing with Language Models</h3>

<h3 id="882">[882] IntroLM: Introspective Language Models via Prefilling-Time Self-Evaluation</h3>

<h3 id="883">[883] Tracing Moral Foundations in Large Language Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2601.05437](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.05437) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Chenxiao Yu, Bowen Yi, Farzan Karimi-Malekabadi, Suhaib Abdurahman, Jinyi Ye, Shrikanth Narayanan, Yue Zhao, Morteza Dehghani

- **Tags**: LLM Alignment, Interpretability, Fairness
- **Summary**: 本文使用道德基础理论作为分析框架，研究了14个LLM中道德基础的编码、组织和表达方式。通过稀疏自编码器和因果干预实验，发现道德概念在LLM中是分布式、分层且部分解耦的，表明多元道德结构可以从语言统计规律性中涌现。

<h3 id="887">[887] AQUA-Bench: Beyond Finding Answers to Knowing When There Are None in Audio Question Answering</h3>

<h3 id="888">[888] A Scalable Entity-Based Framework for Auditing Bias in LLMs</h3>

- **Summary**: 本文提出了一种可扩展的LLM偏见审计框架，使用命名实体作为受控探针来测量模型行为的系统性差异。通过对19亿个数据点的分析，揭示了模型在政治倾向、国家偏好和公司偏好等方面的系统性偏见。

<h3 id="889">[889] Q-learning with Adjoint Matching</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2601.14234](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.14234) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Qiyang Li, Sergey Levine
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.RO; stat.ML
- **Tags**: Reinforcement Learning, Diffusion Model, Optimization
- **Summary**: 本文提出QAM算法，一种基于TD的强化学习方法，通过伴随匹配技术解决了连续动作空间中扩散策略或流匹配策略相对于Q函数的高效优化问题，避免了多步去噪过程中反向传播的数值不稳定性。

<h3 id="890">[890] The Geometric Reasoner: Manifold-Informed Latent Foresight Search for Long-Context Reasoning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2601.18832](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.18832) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Ren Zhuang, Ben Wang, Shuifa Sun
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Reasoning, Long Context, LLM Inference
- **Summary**: 本文提出几何推理器TGR，一种免训练框架，通过流形感知的潜在前瞻搜索来增强长上下文推理。该方法在每个块边界使用轻量级前瞻估计和几何正则化器来评分候选潜在锚点，同时保持内存与块长度呈线性关系。

<h3 id="891">[891] Simulating Complex Multi-Turn Tool Calling Interactions in Stateless Execution Environments</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2601.19914](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.19914) (replaced)

- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.SE
- **Tags**: Tool Learning, Data Synthesis, LLM Agent
- **Summary**: 本文提出DiGiT-TC方法，用于在无状态执行环境中生成多轮工具调用对话的合成数据。该方法通过一种新颖的生成模式，在用户请求中隐式表示某些工具调用，从而生成具有状态环境搜索特征的对话。

<h3 id="892">[892] What's the plan? Metrics for implicit planning in LLMs and their application to rhyme generation and question answering</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2601.20164](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.20164) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Jim Maar, Denis Paperno, Callum Stuart McDougall, Neel Nanda
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: LLM Reasoning, Interpretability, Text Generation
- **Venue**: ICLR 2026
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/Jim-Maar/implicit-planning-in-llms)
- **Summary**: 本文提出了评估LLM隐式规划能力的简单技术，通过押韵诗歌生成和问答的案例研究，证明隐式规划是从1B参数开始就存在的通用机制，并可通过向量操控影响生成过程。

<h3 id="893">[893] Training Reasoning Models on Saturated Problems via Failure-Prefix Conditioning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2601.20829](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.20829) (replaced)

- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: LLM Reasoning, Reinforcement Learning, LLM Training
- **Summary**: 本文提出失败前缀条件化方法，通过在稀有错误轨迹的前缀上进行条件化，将探索转向易失败的推理状态，从而解锁饱和问题中剩余的学习信号，在标准RLVR停滞的地方持续提升性能。

<h3 id="894">[894] On the Overscaling Curse of Parallel Thinking: System Efficacy Contradicts Sample Efficiency</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2601.21619](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.21619) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Yiming Wang, Zhuosheng Zhang, Rui Wang
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: LLM Reasoning, LLM Inference, Optimization
- **Summary**: 本文分析了并行思维中的过度缩放诅咒，即系统效能与样本效率之间的矛盾。提出了潜在预算预测器LanBo，通过探测模型潜在表示来预测样本特定的最优预算，显著提高了预算利用率。

<h3 id="895">[895] Curriculum Learning for LLM Pretraining: An Analysis of Learning Dynamics</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2601.21698](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.21698) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Mohamed Elgaar, Hadi Amiri
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Curriculum Learning, Pre-training, LLM Training
- **Summary**: 本文分析了课程学习对LLM预训练动态的影响，比较了三种语言学驱动的课程。研究发现训练遵循共享的潜在阶段序列，而课程主要改变在每个阶段花费的时间，而非创造新的阶段。

<h3 id="896">[896] Why Adam Works Better with $β_1 = β_2$: The Missing Gradient Scale Invariance Principle</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2601.21739](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.21739) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Alberto Fernández-Hernández, Cristian Pérez-Corral, Jose I. Mestre, Manuel F. Dolz, Enrique S. Quintana-Ortí
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; stat.ML
- **Tags**: Optimization, LLM Training
- **Summary**: 本文解释了Adam优化器在动量参数β1=β2时表现更好的原因，提出了梯度尺度不变性原则。证明了Adam只有在β1=β2时才具有一阶梯度的尺度不变性，为Adam的行为提供了理论解释。

<h3 id="897">[897] Supervised Mixture-of-Experts for Surgical Grasping and Retraction</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2601.21971](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.21971) (replaced)

<h3 id="898">[898] CoDCL: Counterfactual-Inspired Augmentation Contrastive Learning for Temporal Link Prediction in Social Networks</h3>

- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Link Prediction, Social Network Analysis, Contrastive Learning
- **Summary**: 该论文提出了CoDCL框架，结合反事实启发的数据增强和对比学习来解决时序链接预测问题。该方法可以无缝集成到现有的时序图模型中，在多个真实数据集上显著优于现有方法。

<h3 id="899">[899] ScholarPeer: A Context-Aware Multi-Agent Framework for Automated Peer Review</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2601.22638](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.22638) (replaced)

- **Summary**: 该论文介绍了ScholarPeer，一个用于自动化同行评审的多智能体框架，包含领域历史学家、基线搜索器和多方面问答引擎。该框架在约1800篇ICLR投稿上进行了评估，显著优于现有方法。

<h3 id="900">[900] Hyperspherical Autoencoder for High-Fidelity Image Reconstruction and Generation</h3>

- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; cs.LG

- **Summary**: 该论文提出了超球面自编码器(HAE)框架，通过方向特征对齐目标和分层卷积块嵌入模块来桥接语义表示和像素级重建。该方法在球面流形上使用黎曼流匹配训练扩散Transformer，实现了高质量的图像重建和生成。

<h3 id="901">[901] Optimal Transport-Guided Adversarial Attacks on Graph Neural Network-Based Bot Detection</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2602.00318](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.00318) (replaced)

- **Venue**: ICML 2026
- **Summary**: 该论文提出了BOCLOAK方法，通过最优传输引导的对抗攻击来系统评估基于图神经网络的社交机器人检测器的鲁棒性。该方法在真实约束下实现了高达80.13%的攻击成功率提升，同时使用更少的GPU内存。

<h3 id="902">[902] A Minimum Variance Path Principle for Accurate and Stable Score-Based Density Ratio Estimation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2602.00834](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.00834) (replaced)

- **Tags**: Generative Model, Optimization, Density Estimation
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/Hoemr/OpenDRE.git)
- **Summary**: 该论文提出了最小方差路径(MVP)原则来解决基于分数的密度比估计中的路径依赖问题。通过推导方差的闭式表达式并使用Kumaraswamy混合模型参数化路径，该方法实现了更准确和稳定的估计。

<h3 id="903">[903] Calibrating Behavioral Parameters with Large Language Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2602.01022](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.01022) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Brandon Yee, Pairie Koh
- **Subjects**: econ.GN; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Evaluation, Quantitative Finance, Economic Modeling
- **Summary**: 该论文开发了一个将大语言模型作为行为参数校准测量工具的框架，用于测量损失厌恶、羊群效应等行为参数。研究发现基础LLM存在系统性理性偏差，基于配置文件的校准可以产生理论上连贯的参数偏移。

<h3 id="904">[904] Hidden Heroes and Gradient Bloats: Layer-Wise Redundancy Inverts Attribution in Transformers</h3>

- **Summary**: 该论文发现基于梯度的归因方法在Transformer中存在系统性失败：持续高估早期层的梯度膨胀并低估后期层的隐藏英雄。这种失败源于一阶梯度的集体冗余检测能力不足，导致因果重要性的反转。

<h3 id="905">[905] Functional Subspace, where language models can use vector algebra to solve problems</h3>

<h3 id="906">[906] A Physical Theory of Backpropagation: Exact Gradients from the Least-Action Principle</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2602.02281](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.02281) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Antonino Emanuele Scurria
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.NE

<h3 id="907">[907] MathlibLemma: Folklore Lemma Generation and Benchmark for Formal Mathematics</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2602.02561](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.02561) (replaced)

<h3 id="908">[908] What Structural Inductive Bias Helps Transformers Reason Over Knowledge Graphs? A Study with Tabula RASA</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2602.02834](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.02834) (replaced)

<h3 id="909">[909] PrAg-PO: Prompt Augmented Policy Optimization for Robust and Diverse Mathematical Reasoning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2602.03190](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.03190) (replaced)

<h3 id="910">[910] Efficient Estimation of Kernel Surrogate Models for Task Attribution</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2602.03783](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.03783) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Zhenshuo Zhang, Minxuan Duan, Hongyang R. Zhang
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.CL

<h3 id="911">[911] SAR-RAG: ATR Visual Question Answering by Semantic Search, Retrieval, and MLLM Generation</h3>

<h3 id="912">[912] SE-Bench: Benchmarking Self-Evolution with Knowledge Internalization</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2602.04811](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.04811) (replaced)

- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.LG

<h3 id="913">[913] CoreQ: Learning-Free Mismatch Correction and Successive Rounding for Quantization</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2602.05902](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.05902) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Seohyeon Cha, Huancheng Chen, Dongjun Kim, Haoran Zhang, Kevin Chan, Gustavo de Veciana, Haris Vikalo

<h3 id="914">[914] Dynamics-Aligned Shared Hypernetworks for Contextual RL under Discontinuous Shifts</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2602.06550](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.06550) (replaced)

<h3 id="915">[915] Pairwise is Not Enough: Hypergraph Neural Networks for Multi-Agent Pathfinding</h3>

- **Authors**: Rishabh Jain, Keisuke Okumura, Michael Amir, Pietro Lio, Amanda Prorok
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.MA

<h3 id="916">[916] BONSAI: Bayesian Optimization with Natural Simplicity and Interpretability</h3>

- **Summary**: 本文提出BONSAI，一种默认配置感知的贝叶斯优化策略，在保持优化性能的同时最小化与默认配置的偏离。该方法在理论上证明了与GP-UCB相同的无遗憾性质，并能以零获取成本恢复相关坐标集。

<h3 id="917">[917] Breaking the Grid: Distance-Guided Reinforcement Learning in Large Discrete Action Spaces</h3>

- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI

<h3 id="918">[918] SnareNet: Flexible Repair Layers for Neural Networks with Hard Constraints</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2602.09317](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.09317) (replaced)

- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; stat.ML

<h3 id="919">[919] EcoGym: Evaluating LLMs for Long-Horizon Plan-and-Execute in Interactive Economies</h3>

- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI

<h3 id="920">[920] Preventing Rank Collapse in Federated Low-Rank Adaptation with Client Heterogeneity</h3>

- **Authors**: Fei Wu, Jia Hu, Geyong Min, Shiqiang Wang
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.DC
- **Tags**: Federated Learning, Parameter-Efficient Fine-Tuning, LLM Training
- **Summary**: 本文识别并解决了联邦LoRA中的秩崩塌现象，提出raFLoRA方法，通过秩分区聚合来防止全局更新能量集中在最小共享秩上。实验表明该方法在视觉、语言和推理任务上均优于强基线。

<h3 id="921">[921] Formal Policy Enforcement for Real-World Agentic Systems</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2602.16708](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.16708) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Nils Palumbo, Sarthak Choudhary, Jihye Choi, Guy Amir, Prasad Chalasani, Somesh Jha

- **Summary**: 本文提出FORGE框架，基于面向切面编程和Datalog实现智能体系统的形式化策略执行。该框架独立于智能体推理过程执行策略，支持对信息流策略、审批工作流和组织策略的形式化验证。

<h3 id="922">[922] Towards Cross-lingual Values Judgment: A Consensus-Pluralism Perspective</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2602.17283](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.17283) (replaced)

- **Summary**: 本文提出X-Value，首个跨语言价值观判断基准，用于评估LLM在14种语言中对内容深层价值观的判断能力。该基准包含4750个问答对，覆盖7个全球议题类别，揭示了LLM在跨语言价值观判断方面的局限性。

<h3 id="923">[923] Learning from Trials and Errors: Reflective Test-Time Planning for Embodied LLMs</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2602.21198](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.21198) (replaced)

- **Summary**: 本文提出反思式测试时规划方法，整合行动中反思和行动后反思两种模式，使具身LLM能够从错误中学习。该方法在长视界家庭基准和MuJoCo橱柜拟合基准上显著优于基线模型，并实现了向真实机器人的零样本泛化。

<h3 id="924">[924] Exploring the AI Obedience: Why is Generating a Pure Color Image Harder than CyberPunk?</h3>

- **Summary**: 本文识别出生成式AI中的简单性悖论：能够渲染复杂场景的模型往往在生成纯色图像等简单任务上失败。研究提出AI服从性概念框架，并引入Violin基准来评估模型在确定性任务上的像素级精确度。

<h3 id="925">[925] Improving Text-to-Image Generation with Intrinsic Self-Confidence Rewards</h3>

- **Summary**: 本文提出SOLACE框架，使用模型内在的自置信度信号（噪声重建准确度）作为强化学习奖励，无需外部奖励模型或标注数据。该方法在组合生成、文本渲染和图文对齐方面取得了持续改进。

<h3 id="926">[926] TiledAttention: a CUDA Tile SDPA Kernel for PyTorch</h3>

<h3 id="927">[927] ITLC at SemEval-2026 Task 11: Normalization and Deterministic Parsing for Formal Reasoning in LLMs</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2603.02676](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.02676) (replaced)

<h3 id="928">[928] Learning Approximate Nash Equilibria in Cooperative Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning via Mean-Field Subsampling</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2603.03759](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.03759) (replaced)

- **Subjects**: cs.MA; cs.AI; cs.LG; eess.SY; math.OC

<h3 id="929">[929] Relational In-Context Learning via Synthetic Pre-training with Structural Prior</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2603.03805](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.03805) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Yanbo Wang, Jiaxuan You, Chuan Shi, Muhan Zhang
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.DB

<h3 id="930">[930] Upholding Epistemic Agency: A Brouwerian Assertibility Constraint for Responsible AI</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2603.03971](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.03971) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Michael Jülich
- **Subjects**: cs.CY; cs.AI; cs.LG; cs.LO
- **Tags**: AI Ethics, Explainable AI, AI Safety
- **Summary**: 本文提出了一种受Brouwer启发的可断言性约束，要求AI系统在高风险领域只能提供可公开检查和可争议的资格证明时才能做出断言或否认，否则必须返回"未确定"状态。该约束通过三状态接口语义来保护民主认知主体性免受生成式AI过度说服力的影响。

<h3 id="931">[931] What Does Flow Matching Bring To TD Learning?</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2603.04333](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04333) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Bhavya Agrawalla, Michal Nauman, Aviral Kumar
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Reinforcement Learning, Flow Matching, Representation Learning
- **Summary**: 本文分析了流匹配在强化学习TD学习中的成功原因，发现其优势来自于测试时恢复机制和密集速度监督带来的更可塑的特征学习。实验表明流匹配critic在损失可塑性有挑战的场景中显著优于单体critic，最终性能提升2倍，样本效率提升约5倍。

<h3 id="932">[932] From Spark to Fire: Modeling and Mitigating Error Cascades in LLM-Based Multi-Agent Collaboration</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2603.04474](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04474) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Yizhe Xie, Congcong Zhu, Xinyue Zhang, Tianqing Zhu, Dayong Ye, Minfeng Qi, Huajie Chen, Wanlei Zhou
- **Subjects**: cs.MA; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Multi-Agent System, LLM Agent, Error Detection
- **Summary**: 本文提出了针对LLM多智能体系统的错误传播动力学模型，识别出级联放大、拓扑敏感性和共识惯性三类脆弱性，并设计了基于族谱图的治理层来抑制错误放大。该方法在不改变协作架构的情况下，在89%的运行中防止了最终感染。

<h3 id="933">[933] The DSA's Blind Spot: Algorithmic Audit of Advertising and Minor Profiling on TikTok</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2603.05653](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05653) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Sara Solarova, Matej Mosnar, Matus Tibensky, Jan Jakubcik, Adrian Bindas, Simon Liska, Filip Hossner, Matúš Mesarčík, Ivan Srba
- **Subjects**: cs.CY; cs.AI; cs.IR; cs.SI
- **Tags**: Fairness, AI Ethics, Algorithmic Audit
- **Venue**: FAccT 2026
- **Summary**: 本文通过对TikTok的算法审计，揭示了欧盟数字服务法案对"广告"的狭窄定义如何导致未成年人仍暴露于算法定向的商业内容。研究发现TikTok形式上合规但未披露的商业内容对未成年人存在显著的兴趣画像定向，呼吁扩大广告定义以保护未成年人。

<h3 id="934">[934] Towards Batch-to-Streaming Deep Reinforcement Learning for Continuous Control</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2603.08588](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.08588) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Riccardo De Monte, Matteo Cederle, Gian Antonio Susto
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Reinforcement Learning, Sim-to-Real, On-Device Learning
- **Summary**: 本文提出了两种流式深度强化学习算法S2AC和SDAC，专为与最先进的批量RL方法兼容而设计，适用于Sim2Real迁移等设备端微调应用。研究还提出了原则性方法来解决批量到流式转换中预训练策略性能保持的挑战。

<h3 id="935">[935] Gender Fairness in Audio Deepfake Detection: Performance and Disparity Analysis</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2603.09007](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09007) (replaced)

- **Venue**: IEEE CAI 2026
- **Summary**: 本文分析了音频深度伪造检测中的性别公平性问题，使用ASVspoof 5数据集评估了不同音频特征下的检测性能差异。研究发现即使整体错误率差异较小，公平性感知评估仍能揭示聚合指标所掩盖的人口统计学特定失败模式。

<h3 id="936">[936] EvoDriveVLA: Evolving Driving VLA Models via Collaborative Perception-Planning Distillation</h3>

- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Autonomous Driving, Knowledge Distillation, Vision-Language Model
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/hey-cjj/EvoDriveVLA)
- **Summary**: 本文提出了EvoDriveVLA，一种协作感知-规划蒸馏框架，通过自锚定视觉蒸馏和未来感知轨迹蒸馏来解决视觉语言动作模型在自动驾驶中的感知退化和长期规划不稳定问题。该方法在nuScenes和NAVSIM基准上取得了最先进的性能。

<h3 id="937">[937] Where Do Flow Semantics Reside? A Protocol-Native Tabular Pretraining Paradigm for Encrypted Traffic Classification</h3>

- **Subjects**: cs.NI; cs.AI; cs.CR; cs.LG

<h3 id="938">[938] AR-VLA: True Autoregressive Action Expert for Vision-Language-Action Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2603.10126](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10126) (replaced)

- **Tags**: Robotics, Vision-Language Model, Embodied AI
- **Venue**: RSS 2026
- **Summary**: 本文提出了一个自回归动作专家，通过可刷新的视觉语言前缀生成连续因果动作序列，同时通过长期记忆保持上下文感知。该方法解决了快速控制与慢速推理之间的频率不匹配问题，在机器人操作任务中展现出更优的历史感知和更平滑的动作轨迹。

<h3 id="939">[939] OpenClaw-RL: Train Any Agent Simply by Talking</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2603.10165](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10165) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Yinjie Wang, Xuyang Chen, Xiaolong Jin, Mengdi Wang, Ling Yang
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.CV; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Reward Model, Vision-Language Model, Code Generation
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/Gen-Verse/OpenClaw-RL)
- **Summary**: 本文提出了Visual-ERM，一种多模态生成式奖励模型，通过在渲染视觉空间中直接评估视觉到代码质量来提供细粒度、可解释的任务无关反馈。集成到RL后，该方法在图表到代码任务上提升8.4分，并引入了用于判断细粒度图像差异的基准测试。

<h3 id="940">[940] Visual-ERM: Reward Modeling for Visual Equivalence</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2603.13224](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.13224) (replaced)

- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Code Generation, Reinforcement Learning, Vision-Language Model
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/InternLM/Visual-ERM)
- **Summary**: 本文提出了视觉等效奖励模型，一种用于视觉到代码任务的多模态生成式奖励模型，通过在渲染的视觉空间中直接评估代码生成质量来提供细粒度、可解释的任务无关反馈。将该模型集成到强化学习中，在图表转代码、表格解析和SVG解析任务上分别提升了8.4、2.7和4.1分，并发布了用于评估结构化视觉数据细粒度差异的基准测试VC-RewardBench。

<h3 id="941">[941] Robust Building Damage Detection in Cross-Disaster Settings Using Domain Adaptation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2603.14694](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.14694) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Asmae Mouradi, Shruti Kshirsagar
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Domain Adaptation, Remote Sensing, Disaster Response
- **Venue**: IEEE ICHMS 2026
- **Summary**: 本文探索了使用监督域适应进行跨灾害场景建筑损坏分类的两阶段集成方法，解决了训练和部署数据之间的分布不匹配问题。实验表明域适应对损坏检测至关重要，移除它会导致检测完全失败，最佳配置达到0.5552的Macro-F1。

<h3 id="942">[942] Grounding the Score: Explicit Visual Premise Verification for Reliable Vision-Language Process Reward Models</h3>

- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI

- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/Qwen-Applications/EVPV-PRM)

<h3 id="943">[943] VC-Soup: Value-Consistency Guided Multi-Value Alignment for Large Language Models</h3>

<h3 id="944">[944] MolRGen: A Training and Evaluation Setting for De Novo Molecular Generation with Reasonning Models</h3>

- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
